FILE: All_ButtonNmEuHd_lib_01.sha

<?xml version="1.0"?><shapefile version="1" type="dynamic" title="" description="" useFirstShapeForBadValue="0" width="96px" height="24px"><versionheaders><versionheader name="PS" value="6.51"/></versionheaders><stylesheets><stylesheet src="C:\programdata\honeywell\experion pks\client\abstract\CSS\sp.css"/></stylesheets><scripts><script event="onshapeload" language="VBScript" id=""><![CDATA[page.onpagecomplete=getref("page_pagecomplete")]]></script><script event="" language="VBScript" id="46AFBDF0-508A-4C36-8591-40CFE336DC5A"><![CDATA['COPYRIGHT (c) 2004-2010
'HONEYWELL INTL.,
'ALL RIGHTS RESERVED
sub Butt_Page_OnShapeLoad(oSource, sShow)
on error resume next
set txtName = oSource.objects("TxtName")
set txtEUDesc = oSource.objects("TxtEUDesc")
if typename(oSource.objects("ConAlarmState"))<>"Nothing" then 
	sTgNm=oSource.objects("ConAlarmState").datavalue("tagname.name")
elseif oSource.objects("ScConAlpPV").dataexists("tagname.name") then
	sTgNm=oSource.objects("ScConAlpPV").datavalue("tagname.name")
else
	sTgNm=oSource.getcustomproperty("Point","Tagname")
end if


if sShow="Name" then
  txtName.style.visibility="inherit"
  txtEUDesc.style.visibility="hidden"
elseif sShow="EngU" then
  txtName.style.visibility="hidden"
  txtEUDesc.style.visibility="inherit"
else 
  txtName.style.visibility="hidden"
  txtEUDesc.style.visibility="hidden"
end if

sTagEU = oSource.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_TagnameEU") 


if sTagEU <> "-" then
	if sTagEU<>"" then
		if instr(sTagEU,",")<>0 then
			sTag = left(sTagEU,instr(sTagEU,",")-1)
			sEU	= ltrim(mid(sTagEU,instr(sTagEU,",")+1))
			if sTag = "-" and sEU = "" then iSw=2
			if sTag = "-" and (sEU <> "" and sEU <> "-") then iSw=3
			if sTag = "" and sEU = "-" then iSw=4
			if sTag = "" and (sEU <> "" and sEU <> "-") then iSw=6
			if (sTag <> "" and sTag <> "-") and sEU = "-" then iSw=7
			if (sTag <> "" and sTag <> "-") and sEU = "" then iSw=8			
			if (sTag <> "" and sTag <> "-") and (sEU <> "" and sEU <> "-") then iSw=9						
		else
			iSw=7
			sTag=sTagEU
		end if
	else
		iSw=5
	end if				
else
	iSw=1
end if			
			
select case iSw
	case 1: 
		txtName.style.visibility="hidden"
  		txtEUDesc.style.visibility="hidden"	
	case 2:
		txtName.innertext=""
		txtEUDesc.innertext=oSource.Objects("ScConAlpPV").DataValue("tagname.cp_eudesc") 
    case 3:
		txtName.innertext=""
		txtEUDesc.innertext=sEU 
	case 4:
		txtName.innertext=sTgNm
		txtEUDesc.innertext=""
	case 5:
		txtName.innertext=sTgNm
		txtEUDesc.innertext=oSource.Objects("ScConAlpPV").DataValue("tagname.cp_eudesc") 
	case 6:
		txtName.innertext=sTgNm
		txtEUDesc.innertext=sEU 
	case 7:
		txtName.innertext= sTag
		txtEUDesc.innertext=""
	case 8:
		txtName.innertext=sTag
		txtEUDesc.innertext=oSource.Objects("ScConAlpPV").DataValue("tagname.cp_eudesc") 
	case 9:
		txtName.innertext=sTag
		txtEUDesc.innertext=sEU 
end select

end sub


sub page_pagecomplete
	dim oShape
	dim sShow
	on error resume next

	'SP Tagname / EU initialization
	sShow=window.external.dictionary.item("NameUnitsHidden").value
	for each oShape in Page.children.tags("DIV")  
 		if typename(oShape.objects("TxtName"))<>"Nothing" then
    		call Butt_Page_OnShapeLoad(oShape,sShow)
 	end if 
	next
end sub]]></script></scripts><parameters><parameter name="Revision" type="Num" description="" defaultvalue="R400 Rev.0"/></parameters><shapes><shape><group><element><html><content><![CDATA[<INPUT <?xml version="1.0"?><shapefile version="1" type="dynamic" title="" description="" useFirstShapeForBadValue="0" width="103px" height="40px"><versionheaders><versionheader name="PS" value="6.51"/></versionheaders><scripts><script event="" language="VBScript" id="7474A738-1A88-4B95-A8F8-81CB9CA549D1"><![CDATA['COPYRIGHT (c) 2004-2010
'HONEYWELL INTL.,
'ALL RIGHTS RESERVED
'--------------------------------------
'RegCntl - Mode; Redtag; Hialm Type; EUDesc; 
'--------------------------------------
sub CDA_RegCntl_Mode_Hialm_RedTag_EUDesc_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim sMode, oMode, iModeAttr, iNModeAttr, iPID_HialmT, iDac_HialmT, iAlmEnbState, iRedTag, oShape,oPIDAlm,oDACAlm, oInitWindup, iInitMan,iArwop
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if typename(oShape.objects("TxtMode"))<>"Nothing" then
	   set oMode=oShape.objects("TxtMode")
	   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_mode")<>"" then
	      sMode=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_mode")
	      oMode.innertext=left(sMode,1)
	   end if
	   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_nmode")<>"" then
	      sNMode=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_nmode")
		  if sMode=sNMode then
	      	oMode.styleclass="TxtMode_in_NMode"
	      else 
	      	oMode.styleclass="TxtMode_not_in_NMode"
	      end if 
	   end if
	   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_nmodeAttr")<>"" then
		  iNModeAttr=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_nmodeAttr")
		  iModeAttr=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_modeAttr")	
		  if iNModeAttr<>0 then
		   	  if iModeAttr=iNModeAttr then
		      	oMode.linecolor="transparent"
		      else 
		      	oMode.linecolor=oMode.getstyleproperty("mAttrProgF") 
				oMode.linestyle=1
			    oMode.linewidth=oMode.getstyleproperty("lWidth")
		      end if 
			  if iModeAttr=2 then oMode.textcolor = oMode.getstyleproperty("mAttrProgL")
           end if
	   end if		
   end if 
   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_PID_hialm.type")<>"" then
      iPID_HialmT=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_PID_hialm.type")
      set oPIDAlm=oShape.objects("TxtHiAlmTypePIDA")
      select case iPID_HialmT
	     case 0 oPIDAlm.innertext=""
	     case 1 oPIDAlm.innertext="CTL"
	     case 2 oPIDAlm.innertext="SI"
	     case 3 oPIDAlm.innertext="DVH"
	     case 4 oPIDAlm.innertext="DVL"
	     case 5 oPIDAlm.innertext="ADV"
	     case 6 oPIDAlm.innertext="OPH"
	     case 7 oPIDAlm.innertext="OPL"
      end select
   end if
   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_DAC_hialm.type")<>"" then
      iDAC_HialmT=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_DAC_hialm.type")
      set oDACAlm=oShape.objects("TxtHiAlmTypeDACA")
      select case iDAC_HialmT
	     case 0 oDACAlm.innertext=""
	     case 1 oDACAlm.innertext="BAD"
	     case 2 oDACAlm.innertext="HH"
	     case 3 oDACAlm.innertext="LL"
	     case 4 oDACAlm.innertext="H"
	     case 5 oDACAlm.innertext="L"
	     case 6 oDACAlm.innertext="PRC"
	     case 7 oDACAlm.innertext="NRC"
      end select
   end if
   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_redtag")<>"" then
      iRedTag=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_redtag")
      if iRedTag=1 then
         oShape.objects("WdgRedTag").styleclass="WdgRedTag_On"
      else
         oShape.objects("WdgRedTag").styleclass="WdgRedTag_Off"
      end if
   end if
   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_initman")<>"" then
      set oInit = oShape.objects("TxtInit") 
	  set oWindup = oShape.objects("LinWR")
	  iInitMan = oSource.datavalue ("tagname.cp_initman")
	  oWindup.styleclass = "LinWR_No_WindupReset"
	  	if iInitMan = 1 then
			oInit.styleclass = "Init_Active"
			oWindup.styleclass = "LinWR_No_WindupReset"
		else
			oInit.styleclass = "Init_Inactive"
			if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_sptvstate")<>"" then
				iTVstate=oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_sptvstate")
				iTV=oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_sptv")
				if iTVState=2 then
					if typename(oShape.objects("ConAlpSP"))<>"Nothing" then
						rSP=oShape.objects("ConAlpSP").value	
                    else
                        if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_dev")<>"" then
							rSP=oSource.value-oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_dev")
                        else
							rSP=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_sp")
						end if
					end if 	
				    if iTV>rSP then oWindup.styleclass="LinWR_Ramp_HI"
					if iTV<rSP then oWindup.styleclass="LinWR_Ramp_LO"
                else
					oWindup.styleclass="LinWR_No_WindupReset"
				end if
            end if
			if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_arwop")<>"" then
				iArwop = oSource.datavalue ("tagname.cp_arwop")
				select case iArwop
				     case 1 oWindup.styleclass="LinWR_Windup_Hi"
				     case 2 oWindup.styleclass="LinWR_Windup_LO"
				     case 3 oWindup.styleclass="LinWR_Windup_HILO"
				end select
            end if  
		end if
   end if
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_TagnameEU")="" then oShape.objects("TxtEUDesc").innertext = oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_eudesc")
   if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_RegCntl_Mode_Hialm_RedTag_EUDesc_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

'--------------------------------------
'Numeric Values
'--------------------------------------
sub CDA_Numeric_AlpPVFormat_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim sFormat,oShape, nNumericFormat
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   nNumericFormat=oSource.Datavalue("tagname.cp_pvformat") 
   if IsNumeric(nNumericFormat) and instr(nNumericFormat,".")=0 then
	  sFormat="%." & nNumericFormat & "f"
	  if typename(oshape.objects("ScConAlpPV"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").numericdisplayformat=sFormat
	  if typename(oshape.objects("ConAlpSP"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("ConAlpSP").numericdisplayformat=sFormat
   else 
   	  window.external.LogMessage "CDA_Numeric_AlpPVFormat_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & "Invalid PV Format: " & nNumericFormat 
   end if
      if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_Numeric_AlpPVFormat_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

'-------------------------------------
'Controller with Deviation
'--------------------------------------
sub CDA_RegCntl_AlpDev_OnUpdate(oSource)
   on error resume next
   dim rDev, rRange,oShape
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_dev")<>"" then rDev=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_dev")
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_pveuhi")<>"" and oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_pveulo")<>"" then
   		rRange=0.05*(oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_pveuhi")-oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_pveulo"))
   end if
   rRange=csng(oShape.getcustomproperty("Real","RangeDev")) 
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_advdevalm.tp")<>"" then 
   	  if instr(oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_advdevalm.tp"),"N")=0 then	rRange=csng(oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_advdevalm.tp"))
   end if 
   oShape.objects("IndDev").value=(rDev*100/rRange)
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_RegCntl_AlpDev_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

sub CDA_DigStateCText_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape,oPVObj
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   set oPVObj=oShape.objects("TxtPV")
   if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)=iQualityGood then
      oPVObj.innertext=oShape.GetCustomProperty("Text","cp_TextForPV" &  cstr(oSource.value))
      oPVObj.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForPV" & cstr(oSource.value))
   else
      oPVObj.innertext="--------" 
      oPVObj.textcolor=vbRed
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_DigStateCText_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

   if typename(oShape.objects("OvlBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("OvlBody").styleclass="OvlBody_" & index	   
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgBody").styleclass="PlgBody_" & index
   if typename(oShape.objects("RecBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("RecBody").styleclass="RecBody_" & index
   if igPV=cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_BadPVState")) then
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"

      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   end if

end sub

'--------------------------------------
'DevCtl - Redtag; Hialm Type; 
'--------------------------------------
sub CDA_Devctl_Hialm_RedTag_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim iDevctl_HialmT, iRedTag, oShape, oDevctlAlm
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_hialm.type")<>"" then iDevctl_HialmT=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_hialm.type")
   set oDevctlAlm=oShape.objects("TxtHiAlmTypeDEVCTLA")
   select case iDevctl_HialmT
	  case 0 oDevctlAlm.innertext=""
	  case 1 oDevctlAlm.innertext="BAD"
	  case 2 oDevctlAlm.innertext="OFF"
	  case 3 oDevctlAlm.innertext="CMD"
	  case 4 oDevctlAlm.innertext="FL"
	  case 5 oDevctlAlm.innertext="CH"
	  case 6 oDevctlAlm.innertext="SAF"
	  case 7 oDevctlAlm.innertext="OV0"
	  case 8 oDevctlAlm.innertext="OV1"
	  case 9 oDevctlAlm.innertext="OV2"
   end select
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_redtag")<>"" then 
   		iRedTag=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_redtag")
   		if iRedTag=1 then
      		oShape.objects("WdgRedTag").styleclass="WdgRedTag_On"
   		else
      		oShape.objects("WdgRedTag").styleclass="WdgRedTag_Off"
   		end if
   end if 
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_Devctl_Hialm_RedTag_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub CDA_MOVInd_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource) 
   dim oShape,rVal
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   rVal=oSource.value
   if instr(ucase(rVal),"N")=0 then
   		if rVal>100 then 
      		rVal=100
   		elseif rVal<0 then 
      		rVal=0
   		end if
	else
		rVal=100
   end if
   if oShape.objects("TxtOP") is nothing then
	  oShape.objects("IndVal").value=rVal
   else
	  oShape.objects("TxtOP").innertext=round(rVal) & "%"	
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_MOVInd_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

      		oShape.objects("WdgRedTag").styleclass="WdgRedTag_On"
   		else
      		oShape.objects("WdgRedTag").styleclass="WdgRedTag_Off"
   		end if
   end if
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_badctlfl")<>"" then
   		iBadCtlFl=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_badctlfl")
   		if iBadCtlFl=1 then
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   		else
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   		end if
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_RegCtlValves_RedTag_BadCtlFl_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description

   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_pvsts")<>"" then
   		iPvSts = oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_pvsts")
   		if iPvSts = 0 then
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   		else
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   		end if

sub CDA_RegCntlValve_AlpVal_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim rVal,rClosed,oShape, sRange
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   rVal=oSource.value
   rClosed=csng(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","ClosedValue"))
   sRange = lcase(page.GetStyleClassProperty("UseExtended_RegValve_range","key"))
   if sRange <>"yes" then
	   if rVal>100 then 
	      rVal=100
	   elseif rVal<0 then 
	      rVal=0
	   end if
   end if
   if oShape.objects("TxtOP") is nothing then
	  oShape.objects("IndVal").value=rVal
   else
	  oShape.objects("TxtOP").innertext=round(rVal) & "%"	
   end if
   if rVal > rClosed then
	  index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForOpen" )
   else
	  index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForClose" )
   end if
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgIn")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index	   
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgOut")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_RegCntlValve_AlpVal_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description     
end sub

'--------------------------------------
'Bar Shapes 
'--------------------------------------

sub CDA_Bar_OnUpdate(oSource)
    dim oShape,cpRangeHI,cpRangeLO,rangeHI,rangeLO, PriorityLL,PriorityL,PriorityH,PriorityHH
	dim Ind_HH,Ind_H,Ind_L,Ind_LL,sColor,PV,SP,TripHH,TripH,TripL,TripLL,sMode,sNMode
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_mode")<>"" then 
		sMode=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_mode")
		oShape.objects("TxtMode").innertext=left(sMode,1)
    end if
	if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_nmode")<>"" then
      sNMode=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_nmode")
	  if sMode=sNMode then
      	oShape.objects("TxtMode").styleclass="TxtMode_in_NMode"
      else 
      	oShape.objects("TxtMode").styleclass="TxtMode_not_in_NMode"
      end if 
    end if
    rangeHI=oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_pveuhi")
    rangeLO=oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_pveulo")
	cpRangeHI=oShape.getcustomproperty("Real","cp_rangeHI")
	if cpRangeHI="" then cpRangeHI=rangeHI
    cpRangeLO=oShape.getcustomproperty("Real","cp_rangeLO")
	if cpRangeLO="" then cpRangeLO=rangeLO
    PV=oSource.value
	if csng(PV)<csng(cpRangeLO) then PV=cpRangeLO
	if csng(PV)>csng(cpRangeHI) then PV=cpRangeHI
    oShape.objects("IndPV").value=(PV-cpRangeLO)/(cpRangeHI-cpRangeLO)*100
    if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_sp")<>"" and typename(oShape.objects("IndSP"))<>"Nothing" then
		SP=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_sp")
		if instr(SP,"N")=0 then 
			oShape.objects("IndSP").style.visibility="inherit"
			if csng(SP)<csng(cpRangeLO) then SP=cpRangeLO
			if csng(SP)>csng(cpRangeHI) then SP=cpRangeHI
    		oShape.objects("IndSP").value=(SP-cpRangeLO)/(cpRangeHI-cpRangeLO)*100
        else
			oShape.objects("IndSP").style.visibility="hidden"
		end if 
    end if
	if typename(oshape.objects("IndHH"))<>"Nothing" and typename(oshape.objects("IndH"))<>"Nothing" and typename(oshape.objects("IndL"))<>"Nothing" and typename(oshape.objects("IndLL"))<>"Nothing" then	
		set Ind_HH=oShape.objects("IndHH")
		set Ind_H=oShape.objects("IndH")
		set Ind_L=oShape.objects("IndL")
		set Ind_LL=oShape.objects("IndLL")
		PriorityHH=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvhhalm.pr")
		if PriorityHH<>"JOURNAL" and PriorityHH<>"NONE" then
		    sColor="AlarmStateIconFactory." & PriorityHH & "Color"
		    Ind_HH.levelfillcolor=eval(sColor)
			Ind_HH.Style.visibility="inherit"
			TripHH=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvhhalm.tp")
			if csng(TripHH)>csng(cpRangeHI) then TripHH=cpRangeHI
			if csng(TripHH)<csng(cpRangeLO) then TripHH=cpRangeLO  
   		    oShape.objects("IndHH").value=100-(TripHH-cpRangeLO)/(cpRangeHI-cpRangeLO)*100
		end if    
		PriorityH=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvhialm.pr")
		if PriorityH<>"JOURNAL" and PriorityH<>"NONE" then
		    sColor="AlarmStateIconFactory." & PriorityH & "Color"
		    Ind_H.levelfillcolor=eval(sColor)
			Ind_H.Style.visibility="inherit"
			TripH=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvhialm.tp")
			if csng(TripH)>csng(cpRangeHI) then TripH=cpRangeHI
			if csng(TripH)<csng(cpRangeLO) then TripH=cpRangeLO  
	        oShape.objects("IndH").value=100-(TripH-cpRangeLO)/(cpRangeHI-cpRangeLO)*100
		end if
		PriorityL=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvloalm.pr")
		if PriorityL<>"JOURNAL" and PriorityL<>"NONE" then
		    sColor="AlarmStateIconFactory." & PriorityL & "Color"
		    Ind_L.levelfillcolor=eval(sColor)
			Ind_L.Style.visibility="inherit"
			TripL=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvloalm.tp")
			if csng(TripL)>csng(cpRangeHI) then TripL=cpRangeHI
			if csng(TripL)<csng(cpRangeLO) then TripL=cpRangeLO  
		    oShape.objects("IndL").value=(TripL-cpRangeLO)/(cpRangeHI-cpRangeLO)*100
		end if
		PriorityLL=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvllalm.pr")
		if PriorityLL<>"JOURNAL" and PriorityLL<>"NONE" then
		    sColor="AlarmStateIconFactory." & PriorityLL & "Color"
		    Ind_LL.levelfillcolor=eval(sColor)
			Ind_LL.Style.visibility="inherit"
			TripLL=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvllalm.tp")
			if csng(TripLL)>csng(cpRangeHI) then TripLL=cpRangeHI
			if csng(TripLL)<csng(cpRangeLO) then TripLL=cpRangeLO  
		    oShape.objects("IndLL").value=(TripLL-cpRangeLO)/(cpRangeHI-cpRangeLO)*100
		end if
	end if
	if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_badctlfl")<>"" then
   		iBadCtlFl=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_badctlfl")
   		if iBadCtlFl=1 then
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   		else
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   		end if
    elseif oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_pvsts")<>"" then 
        iPvSts = oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_pvsts")
   		if iPvSts = 0 then
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   		else
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   		end if 
    end if
    if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CDA_Bar_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub CDA_DevBar_OnUpdate(oSource)
    dim oShape,cpRange,DevHI,DevLO,PriorityDevHI,PriorityDevLO
	dim Ind_DevHI,Ind_DevLO,sColor,DEV,TripDevHI,TripDevLO
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	sMode=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_mode")
	oShape.objects("TxtMode").innertext=left(sMode,1)
	if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_nmode")<>"" then
      sNMode=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_nmode")
	  if sMode=sNMode then
      	oShape.objects("TxtMode").styleclass="TxtMode_in_NMode"
      else 
      	oShape.objects("TxtMode").styleclass="TxtMode_not_in_NMode"
      end if 
    end if 
	cpRange=oShape.getcustomproperty("Real","cp_range")
	if cpRange="" then cpRange=(oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pveuhi")-oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pveulo"))*0.1 
    DEV=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_dev")
	oShape.objects("IndDEV").value=DEV*100/cpRange
	if typename(oshape.objects("IndDevH"))<>"Nothing"  and typename(oshape.objects("IndDevL"))<>"Nothing" then
		set Ind_DevHI=oShape.objects("IndDevH")
		set Ind_DevLO=oShape.objects("IndDevL")
		PriorityDevHI=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_devhialm.pr") 
		if PriorityDevHI<>"JOURNAL" and PriorityDevHI<>"NONE" then
	    	sColor="AlarmStateIconFactory." & PriorityDevHI & "Color"
	    	Ind_DevHI.levelfillcolor=eval(sColor)
			Ind_DevHI.Style.visibility="inherit"
			TripDevHI=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_devhialm.tp")
		end if    
    	PriorityDevLO=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_devloalm.pr")
		if PriorityDevLO<>"Journal" and PriorityDevLO<>"" then
	    	sColor="AlarmStateIconFactory." & PriorityDevLO & "Color"
	    	Ind_DevLO.levelfillcolor=eval(sColor)
			Ind_DevLO.Style.visibility="inherit"
			TripDevLO=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_devloalm.tp")
		end if
		Ind_DevHI.value=(cpRange-TripDevHI)*100/cpRange
    	Ind_DevLO.value=(cpRange-abs(TripDevLO))*100/cpRange
	end if	
	if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_badctlfl")<>"" then
   		iBadCtlFl=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_badctlfl")
   		if iBadCtlFl=1 then
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   		else
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   		end if
    end if	
    if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CDA_DevBar_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'--------------------------------------------
' Switches
'--------------------------------------------
sub CDA_Switch_AlpGPV_OnUpdate(oSource) 
   dim igPV,oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   igPV=oSource.value
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & igPV)
   if typename(oShape.objects("LinSw0")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("LinSw0").styleclass="LinSw0_" & index	   
   if typename(oShape.objects("LinSw1")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("LinSw1").styleclass="LinSw1_" & index	   
   if igPV=cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_BadPVState")) then
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   else
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_Switch_AlpGPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description    
end sub

sub CDA_Switch3P_AlpPAR_OnUpdate(oSource) 
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	Set PolTL = oSource.ParentNode.ParentNode.Objects("PolTL")
	Set PolTR = oSource.ParentNode.ParentNode.Objects("PolTR")
	Set PolBL = oSource.ParentNode.ParentNode.Objects("PolBL")
	Set PolBR = oSource.ParentNode.ParentNode.Objects("PolBR")
	Set LinH = oSource.ParentNode.ParentNode.Objects("LinH")
	Set LinV = oSource.ParentNode.ParentNode.Objects("LinV")
	PolTL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_Off"
	PolTR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_Off"
	PolBL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_Off"
	PolBR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_Off"
	LinH.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_Off"
	LinV.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_Off"
	for i=1 to 3 
   		If ucase(oSource.value) = ucase(oShape.GetCustomProperty("Text", "cp_Value" & i)) or i=3  Then        	
			Select case UCase(oShape.GetCustomProperty("Text", "cp_Position" & i))
				Case "TL"
					PolTL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "TR"
					PolTR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "BL"
					PolBL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "BR"
					PolBR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "H"
					LinH.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "V"
					LinV.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "TLBR"
					PolTL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					PolBR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "TRBL"
					PolTR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					PolBL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case ""
				Case Else
					PolTL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					PolTR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					PolBL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					PolBR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					LinH.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					LinV.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"			
			end select
			exit for
		end if
	next		 
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_Switch3P_AlpPAR_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description    
end sub

'--------------------------------------
'Totalizers; 
'--------------------------------------
sub CDA_Totalizer_TVDEV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape
   on error resume next
   iTV=oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_acctv")
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_acctvfl")=0 then
   		for i = 1 to 4 
   			if oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_accdevfl" & i)=1 and oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_acctp" & i)< iTV then
				iTV = oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_acctp" & i)
				iInd=i
			end if
   		next
		oShape.objects("TxtAccTvDevFl").innertext = oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_AccDev" & iInd &"Text")
	else
		oShape.objects("TxtAccTvDevFl").innertext = oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_AccTvText")
	end if
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_TagnameEU")="" then oShape.objects("TxtEUDesc").innertext = oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_eudesc")
   if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_Totalizer_TVDEV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub


sub CDA_Procop_Alp_OnUpdate(oSource)
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	state=oSource.value
	exsts=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_execsts")
	instpend=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_instpend")
	
	set oSeqState=oSHape.objects("seqState")
	set oSeqSts=oSHape.objects("seqExecSts")
	set oFrame=oSHape.objects("PlgFrame")
	
	if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then 
		oSeqState.value =2
	else
		oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame_Running"
		select case state
			case 1,2,7,3,6 oSeqState.value=1
						 oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame"	
			case 4,11,10 oSeqState.value=3  			 	
			case 8 oSeqState.value=4  
			case 12 oSeqState.value=5 			 
			case 14 oSeqState.value=6
			case 16 oSeqState.value=7
			case 9 oSeqState.value=8
			case 13 oSeqState.value=9
			case 15 oSeqState.value=10
			case 17 oSeqState.value=11
			case 5 oSeqState.value=12
			       oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame"
			case 18 oSeqState.value=2
					oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame"
	    end select 
	end if

    oSeqSts.value=1
    if instpend=1 then oSeqSts.value=2
    select case exsts
		case 2, 8 oSeqSts.value=3
        case 3, 9 oSeqSts.value=4 
		case 5, 11 oSeqSts.value=5
		case 4, 10 oSeqSts.value=6
    end select

	if oShape.style.pixelwidth <>196 then
		iLeft = oShape.objects("ScTxtSelectBox").style.pixelleft
		iWidth = oShape.objects("ScTxtSelectBox").style.pixelwidth
		oShape.objects("RecFrame").style.pixelleft = iLeft+2
		oShape.objects("RecFrame").style.pixelwidth = iWidth-4
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").style.pixelleft = iLeft+6
		iY1 = oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(1).y
		iY2 = oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(2).y
		iY3 = oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(3).y
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(3).setnodepoint iWidth-18,iY3
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(2).setnodepoint iWidth-6,iY2
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(1).setnodepoint iWidth-18,iY1
		oShape.objects("seqState").style.pixelleft = iLeft+7
		oShape.objects("seqState").style.pixelwidth = 16
		oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").style.pixelleft = iLeft+iWidth-33
		oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").style.pixelwidth = 22
		oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelleft = iLeft+iWidth-56
		oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelwidth = 16
		oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelleft = iLeft+24
		oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelwidth = oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelleft-oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelleft
	end if
	if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CDA_Procop_Alp_OnUpdate->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

sub CDA_ProcopStep_Alp_OnUpdate(oSource)
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	state=oSource.value
	stName = oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_BlockName")
	sDV = "tagname." & StName 
	exsts=oSource.datavalue(sDV & ".execsts")
	instpend=oSource.datavalue(sDV & ".instructpend")
	swait = oSource.datavalue(sDV & ".posttransexecsts")
	swarn = oSource.datavalue(sDV & ".warning")
	completed = oSource.datavalue(sDV & ".completed")

	set oSeqState=oSHape.objects("seqState")
	set oSeqSts=oSHape.objects("seqExecSts")
	set oFrame=oSHape.objects("PlgFrame")

	if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then 
		oSeqState.value =2
	else
		oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame_Running"
		select case state
			case 1,2 	oSeqState.value=1
						oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame"	
			case 4,6 	oSeqState.value=3  			 	
			case 5 		oSeqState.value=13  	
	    end select 
		if completed = 1 then oSeqState.value=12
		if csng(swait) < 12 and state <> 1 and state <>2 then oSeqState.value=14
	end if

    oSeqSts.value=1
    if instpend=1 then oSeqSts.value=2
    select case exsts
		case 2, 8 oSeqSts.value=3
        case 3, 9 oSeqSts.value=4 
		case 5, 11 oSeqSts.value=5
		case 4, 10 oSeqSts.value=6
    end select
	if len(trim(swarn))>1 then
		oShape.objects("picWarn").style.visibility = "visible"
	else
		oShape.objects("picWarn").style.visibility = "hidden"
	end if
	
	if oShape.style.pixelwidth <>204 then
		iLeft = oShape.objects("ScTxtSelectBox").style.pixelleft
		iWidth = oShape.objects("ScTxtSelectBox").style.pixelwidth
		oShape.objects("RecFrame").style.pixelleft = iLeft+2
		oShape.objects("RecFrame").style.pixelwidth = iWidth-4
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").style.pixelleft = iLeft+6
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").style.pixelwidth = iWidth-12
		oShape.objects("seqState").style.pixelleft = iLeft+7
		oShape.objects("seqState").style.pixelwidth = 16
		oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").style.pixelleft = iLeft+iWidth-26
		oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").style.pixelwidth = 22
		oShape.objects("picWarn").style.pixelleft = iLeft+iWidth-39
		oShape.objects("picWarn").style.pixelwidth = 16
		oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelleft = iLeft+iWidth-56
		oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelwidth = 16
		oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelleft = iLeft+24
		oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelwidth = oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelleft-oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelleft
	end if
	if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CDA_ProcopStep_Alp_OnUpdate->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

sub CDA_ProcopPhase_Alp_OnUpdate(oSource)

	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode

	state=oSource.value
	phName = oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_BlockName")
	sDV = "tagname." & phName 
	exsts=oSource.datavalue(sDV & ".execsts")
	prLevel = oSource.datavalue("Tagname.procedurallevel")
	chModeAttr = oSource.datavalue(sDV & ".equipmodeattr")
	completed = oSource.datavalue(sDV & ".completed")
	acqState = oSource.datavalue(sDV & ".acqstate")

	set oSeqState=oSHape.objects("seqState")
	set oSeqSts=oSHape.objects("seqExecSts")
	set oFrame=oSHape.objects("PlgFrame")
	set oSeqPLevel = oShape.objects("seqLevel")
	if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then 
		oSeqState.value =2
	else
		oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame_Running"
		select case state
			case 1				oSeqState.value=1
								oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame"	
			case 4,11,12,14 	oSeqState.value=3  	'Active		 	
			case 5 				oSeqState.value=13  
	    end select 
		if completed = 1 then oSeqState.value=12
	end if
    oSeqSts.value=1
    select case exsts
		case 2, 8 oSeqSts.value=3
        case 3, 9 oSeqSts.value=4 
		case 5, 11 oSeqSts.value=5
		case 4, 10 oSeqSts.value=6
		case else oSeqSts.value=0
    end select

	select case prLevel
		case 0 oSeqPLevel.value =5
		case 1 oSeqPLevel.value =1
		case 2 oSeqPLevel.value =2
		case 3 oSeqPLevel.value =3
		case 4 oSeqPLevel.value =4
	end select

	if chModeAttr <>2 or acqState =1 or acqState =4 or acqState =5 then oSeqSts.value = 7 'Child modeattribute not PROGRAM

	if oShape.style.pixelwidth <>204 then
		iLeft = oShape.objects("ScTxtSelectBox").style.pixelleft
		iWidth = oShape.objects("ScTxtSelectBox").style.pixelwidth
		oShape.objects("RecFrame").style.pixelleft = iLeft+2
		oShape.objects("RecFrame").style.pixelwidth = iWidth-4
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").style.pixelleft = iLeft+6
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").style.pixelwidth = iWidth-12
		oShape.objects("seqState").style.pixelleft = iLeft+7
		oShape.objects("seqState").style.pixelwidth = 16
		oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").style.pixelleft = iLeft+iWidth-26
		oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").style.pixelwidth = 22
		oShape.objects("oSeqPLevel").style.pixelleft = iLeft+iWidth-39
		oShape.objects("oSeqPLevel").style.pixelwidth = 16
		oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelleft = iLeft+iWidth-56
		oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelwidth = 16
		oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelleft = iLeft+24
		oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelwidth = oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelleft-oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelleft
	end if
	if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CDA_ProcopPhase_Alp_OnUpdate->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub CDA_ProcopTrans_Alp_OnUpdate(oSource)

	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	blName = oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_BlockName")
	sDV = "tagname." & blName 
	exsts=oSource.datavalue(sDV & ".execsts")
	state=oSource.value
	completed = oSource.datavalue(sDV & ".processed")
	
	set oSeqState=oSHape.objects("seqState")
	set oSeqSts=oSHape.objects("seqExecSts")
	set oFrame=oSHape.objects("PlgFrame")

	if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then 
		oSeqState.value =2
	else
		oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame_Running"
		select case state
			case 1,2,6,7 oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame"	
				if completed = 1 then 
					oSeqState.value=12	
				else
					oSeqState.value=1
				end if
			case 3,4,11,10 oSeqState.value=3  			 	
	    end select 
	    oSeqSts.value=1
	end if
    select case exsts
		case 2, 8 oSeqSts.value=3
       case 3, 9 oSeqSts.value=4 
		case 5, 11 oSeqSts.value=5
		case 4, 10 oSeqSts.value=6
    end select

	if oShape.style.pixelwidth <>300 then
		iLeft = oShape.objects("ScTxtSelectBox").style.pixelleft
		iWidth = oShape.objects("ScTxtSelectBox").style.pixelwidth
		oShape.objects("RecFrame").style.pixelleft = iLeft+2
		oShape.objects("RecFrame").style.pixelwidth = iWidth-4
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").style.pixelleft = iLeft+6
		iY1 = oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(1).y
		iY2 = oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(2).y
		iY3 = oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(3).y
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(3).setnodepoint iWidth-6,iY3
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(2).setnodepoint iWidth-18,iY2
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(1).setnodepoint iWidth-6,iY1
		oShape.objects("seqState").style.pixelleft = iLeft+7
		oShape.objects("seqState").style.pixelwidth = 16
		oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").style.pixelleft = iLeft+iWidth-38
		oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").style.pixelwidth = 22
		oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelleft = iLeft+iWidth-54
		oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelwidth = 16
		oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelleft = iLeft+24
		oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelwidth = oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelleft-oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelleft
	end if
	if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CDA_ProcopTrans_Alp_OnUpdate->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub CDA_ProcopInstr_Alp_OnUpdate(oSource)
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	state=oSource.value
	stName = oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_BlockName")
	sDV = "tagname." & StName 
	exsts=oSource.datavalue(sDV & ".execsts")
	instpend=oSource.datavalue(sDV & ".instructpend")
	completed = oSource.datavalue(sDV & ".completed")
	desc = oSource.datavalue(sDV & ".op[1].desc")

	set oSeqState=oSHape.objects("seqState")
	set oSeqSts=oSHape.objects("seqExecSts")
	set oFrame=oSHape.objects("PlgFrame")

	if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then 
		oSeqState.value =2
	else
		oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame_Running"
		select case state
			case 1,2 	oSeqState.value=1
						oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame"	
			case 4,6 	oSeqState.value=3  			 	 	
	    end select 
		if instpend=1 then 
			oSeqState.value=4 
		else
			if completed = 1 then oSeqState.value=3
		end if
	end if
	oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").value = desc
    oSeqSts.value=1
    select case exsts
		case 2, 8 oSeqSts.value=3
        case 3, 9 oSeqSts.value=4 
		case 5, 11 oSeqSts.value=5
		case 4, 10 oSeqSts.value=6
    end select
		
	if oShape.style.pixelwidth <>300 then
		iLeft = oShape.objects("ScTxtSelectBox").style.pixelleft
		iWidth = oShape.objects("ScTxtSelectBox").style.pixelwidth
		oShape.objects("RecFrame").style.pixelleft = iLeft+2
		oShape.objects("RecFrame").style.pixelwidth = iWidth-4
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").style.pixelleft = iLeft+6
		iY1 = oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(1).y
		iY2 = oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(2).y
		iY3 = oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(3).y
		iY4 = oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(4).y
		iY5 = oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(5).y
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(5).setnodepoint iWidth-6,iY5
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(4).setnodepoint iWidth-12,iY4
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(3).setnodepoint iWidth-6,iY3
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(2).setnodepoint iWidth-12,iY2
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(1).setnodepoint iWidth-6,iY1
		oShape.objects("seqState").style.pixelleft = iLeft+7
		oShape.objects("seqState").style.pixelwidth = 16
		oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").style.pixelleft = iLeft+iWidth-38
		oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").style.pixelwidth = 22
		oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelleft = iLeft+iWidth-54
		oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelwidth = 16
		oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelleft = iLeft+24
		oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelwidth = oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelleft-oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelleft
	end if
	if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CDA_ProcopInstr_Alp_OnUpdate->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'-------------------------------------------------------
'Louvers
'-------------------------------------------------------
sub CDA_Louvers_AlpGPV_OnUpdate(oSource) 
   dim igPV,oShape,igOP,index,indexOP
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   igPV=oSource.value
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & igPV)
   if typename(oShape.objects("LnClose")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("LnClose").styleclass="LnCLose_" & index	   
   if typename(oShape.objects("LnOpen")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("LnOpen").styleclass="LnOpen_" & index
   if typename(oShape.objects("LvrInbet")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("LvrInbet").styleclass="LvrInbet_" & index
   if typename(oShape.objects("LvrBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("LvrBody").styleclass="LvrBody_" & index 	
   if igPV=cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_BadPVState")) then
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   else
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   end if
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_gop")<>"" then
   		igOP = oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_gop")
		indexOP = oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & igOP)
		if typename(oShape.objects("LvrMFold")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("LvrMfold").styleclass = "LvrMfold_" & indexOP
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_Louvers_AlpGPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'-------------------------------------------------------
'MO Fans
'-------------------------------------------------------
sub CDA_MOFan_AlpGPV_OnUpdate(oSource) 
   dim igPV,oShape,igOP,index,indexOP
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   igPV=oSource.value
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & igPV)
   if typename(oShape.objects("OvlBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("OvlBody").styleclass="OvlBody_" & index	   
   if igPV=cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_BadPVState")) then
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   else

   end if

'This shape provides the Solution Pack Context Menu functionality.
'All subroutines are called within SP context menu files

'Acknowledge the alarm on currently selected point
sub acknowledge ()
	window.external.invokemenu2 "Acknowledge Alarm"
end sub

'Invoke detail display of currently selected point 
sub detail ()
	window.external.invokemenu2("Request Point Detail")
end sub

'Invoke the trend group where currently selected point is configured or prompt for trend group number.
sub trd_invoke ()
	on error resume next
	dim oshape, custtrd
	set oshape = document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	custtrd=oshape.getcustomproperty("num","cp_custtrd")
	if custtrd<>"" then 
		window.external.requestserverlrn 21,8,custtrd
	else
		window.external.invokemenu2("request trend")
	end if			
	if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "trd_invoke ->" & oShape.ID & " " & err.description 
end sub

'Invoke the group where currently selected point is configured or prompt for group number.
sub grp_invoke ()
	on error resume next
	dim oshape, custgrp
	set oshape = document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	custgrp = oshape.getcustomproperty("num","cp_custgrp")
	if custgrp <> "" then
		window.external.requestserverlrn 21,49,custgrp
	else
		window.external.invokemenu2("request group")
	end if
	if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "grp_invoke ->" & oShape.ID & " " & err.description 
end sub

'Invoke associated display for currently selected point
sub ascdsp ()
	window.external.invokemenu2("associated display")
end sub

'Open help file using Windows Explorer, if configured in cm_file custom property
sub help ()
	on error resume next
	dim oshape, spath, cm_file
	set oshape = document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	cm_file = oshape.getcustomproperty("text","cm_file")
	if instr(cm_file,"\")>0 then
 		window.external.shell "explorer.exe " & cm_file,1
	else
 		spath = page.document.url
 		spath=mid (spath,8,len(spath))
 		spath=mid (spath,1,instrrev(spath,"\"))  & "helpfiles\" & cm_file
 		spath = Replace(spath,"%20"," ")
 		window.external.shell "explorer.exe " & spath,1
	end if
	if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "help ->" & oShape.ID & " " & err.description 
end sub

'Open Support Display if Configured in "cp_SupDsp_File" custom property
sub SupDsp ()
	on error resume next
	dim oshape, spath, cm_file
	set oshape = document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	cm_file = oshape.getcustomproperty("text","cp_SupDsp_File")
	set oShape=document.getelementbyid(oSource.id).parentnode.parentnode
	sname=oShape.getcustomproperty("Point","tagname") 'in case sname below returns "" 
	if typename(oShape.objects("ConAlarmState"))<>"Nothing" then
		sname=oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").datavalue("tagname.name")
    elseif typename(oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV"))<>"Nothing" then
		sname=oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").datavalue("tagname.name")
    end if
	if cm_file<>"" then window.external.currentpage=cm_file & "?currentpoint=" & sname 
	if err.number <> 0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "SupDsp ->" & oShape.ID & " " & err.description 
end sub

'Called in OnPage event of the Help Item of the Context Menu. Manipulates the menu item text.
sub pageload(osource)
	on error resume next
	dim oshape, spath, cm_file
	set oshape = document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	cm_label = oshape.getcustomproperty("text","cm_label")
	if (cm_label <> "") then	
		oSource.all("text").innertext=" " & cm_label	
		cm_file=oshape.getcustomproperty("text","cm_file")
		if cm_file="" then osource.disabled=true		
	end if
	if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "pageload ->" & oShape.ID & " " & oSource & " " & err.description 
end sub

'Called in OnPage event of the Support Display Item of the Context Menu. Manipulates the menu item text.
sub pageloadSupDsp(osource)
	on error resume next
	dim oshape, spath, cm_file
	set oshape = document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	cm_label = oshape.getcustomproperty("text","cp_SupDsp_Label")
	if (cm_label <> "") then	
		oSource.all("text").innertext=" " & cm_label	
		cm_file=oshape.getcustomproperty("text","cp_SupDsp_File")
		if cm_file="" then osource.disabled=true		
	end if
	if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "pageloadSupDsp ->" & oShape.ID & " " & oSource & " " & err.description 
end sub


' Enable/Disable Add Traces to Trend functionality 
sub AddTrdCnfg (oSource, sText)
	On Error Resume Next
	dim oShape, spath, cm_param
	if isnull(page.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH")) then 
   		oSource.disabled = True
  		exit sub
	end if
	set oShape = document.getElementById(page.getattribute("LastSelection")).parentnode.parentnode
	cm_param = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter",sText)
	if cm_param = "" then oSource.disabled = True
	if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "AddTrdCnfg ->" & oShape.ID & " " & oSource & " "& sText & " " & err.description 
end sub

'Enable/Disable View Trend Configuration functionality. Called in OnPage event of View Trend Item
Sub View_Trd_Load(oSource)
	on error resume next
	if isnull(page.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH")) or isnull(page.getattribute("TrdSel"))  then oSource.disabled = True
    if oSOurce.id="mnu_View_TrdCnf" then
		for each oObj in page.all
	   		if instr(ucase(oObj.classname),"HSC.TREND.1")<>0 then oSource.disabled = True
		next
	end if	  
end sub

'View Trend Configuration if enabled in View_Trd_Load
sub view_trd
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
	set oshape=document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH"))
	if oshape.objects("PbHide").style.visibility="visible" then oshape.objects("PbHide").click
	oshape.style.zindex=5000
	iPageWidth = page.style.pixelwidth
	iSelectedShapeX = document.activeElement.parentnode.parentnode.style.pixelleft
	if iSelectedShapeX > iPageWidth/2 then
		iTableLeft = 20
	else
		iTableLeft = iPageWidth - 420
	end if
	oshape.style.pixelleft=iTableLeft
	oshape.style.pixeltop=80
	oshape.objects("trdtbl").style.visibility="visible"
	oshape.objects("trdtbl").style.pixelwidth=400
	oshape.objects("PbHide").style.visibility="visible"
	oshape.objects("PbHide").style.pixelleft=oshape.objects("PbHide").style.pixelleft+336
	if oShape.objects("alpisprotected").value = 1 and window.external.securitylevel<4 then oshape.objects("RecProtected").style.pixelwidth=400	
	if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "view_trd ->" & oShape.ID & " " & oSource & " " & err.description 
end sub

'Invoke mini trend popup
sub view_mini_trend
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
	set oShape = document.getElementById(page.getattribute("LastSelection")).parentnode.parentnode    
	Trd_ParSP= oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter","Trd_PointSP")
	DotLoc= Instr(1, Trd_ParSP,".")
	sPointSP_CP= Mid(Trd_ParSP,1, DotLoc-1)
	sParameterSP_CP = Mid(Trd_ParSP, DotLoc+1, 24)
	cm_pointSP = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Point",sPointSP_CP)
	if cm_pointSP = "" then cm_pointSP = sPointSP_cp
	cm_paramSP = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter",sParameterSP_CP)
	if cm_paramSP = "" then cm_paramSP = sParameterSP_CP
	Trd_ParPV= oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter","Trd_PointPV")
	DotLoc= Instr(1, Trd_ParPV,".")
	sPointPV_CP= Mid(Trd_ParPV,1, DotLoc-1)
	sParameterPV_CP = Mid(Trd_ParPV, DotLoc+1, 24)
	cm_pointPV = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Point",sPointPV_CP)
	if cm_pointPV = "" then cm_pointPV = sPointPV_cp
	cm_paramPV = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter",sParameterPV_CP)
	if cm_paramPV = "" then cm_paramPV = sParameterPV_CP
    Trd_ParOP= oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter","Trd_PointOP")
	DotLoc= Instr(1, Trd_ParOP,".")
	sPointOP_CP= Mid(Trd_ParOP,1, DotLoc-1)
	sParameterOP_CP = Mid(Trd_ParOP, DotLoc+1, 24)
	cm_pointOP = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Point",sPointOP_CP)
	if cm_pointOP = "" then cm_pointOP = sPointOP_cp
	cm_paramOP = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter",sParameterOP_CP)
	if cm_paramOP = "" then cm_paramOP = sParameterOP_CP
	xcoord=page.getstyleclassproperty("Mini_Trend","left_position")
	ycoord=page.getstyleclassproperty("Mini_Trend","top_position")
	set oSview=getobject("","Honeywell.Workspace.Client")
	If oSview.IsManagerActive = false then
		if xcoord="default" or ycoord="default" then
			hdxpopupbehavior.createpopupwindow "Mini_Trend_Popup.htm?currentpoint=minitrd"& round (rnd*10000) & "&Point1=" & cm_pointPV & "&Point2=" & cm_pointSP & "&Point3=" & cm_pointOP & "&Parameter1=" & cm_paramPV & "&Parameter2=" & cm_paramSP & "&Parameter3="& cm_paramOP, 0, 0, 1
		else 
			set oMini_trd=HdxPopupBehavior.FindPopup("minitrd")
			if typename(oMini_trd)<>"Nothing" then oMini_trd.close
			hdxpopupbehavior.createpopupwindow "Mini_Trend_Popup.htm?currentpoint=minitrd"& round (rnd*10000) & "&Point1=" & cm_pointPV & "&Point2=" & cm_pointSP & "&Point3=" & cm_pointOP & "&Parameter1=" & cm_paramPV & "&Parameter2=" & cm_paramSP & "&Parameter3="& cm_paramOP, xcoord, ycoord, 0
		end if
    else
		hdxpopupbehavior.createpopupwindow "Mini_Trend_Popup.htm?currentpoint=minitrd"& round (rnd*10000) & "&Point1=" & cm_pointPV & "&Point2=" & cm_pointSP & "&Point3=" & cm_pointOP & "&Parameter1=" & cm_paramPV & "&Parameter2=" & cm_paramSP & "&Parameter3="& cm_paramOP, 0, 0, 1
	end if
end sub

' Invoke trend popup. called within sp_systrdsel_sm.xml
sub trd_popup
	on error resume next
	set oShape = document.getElementById(page.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH"))
	sFP = "Chart_Popup_CP.htm?currentpoint=dummy" & oShape.objects("AlpTrendGrp").value
	for i = 1 to 32
		point = oshape.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Point.value").getvalue(i-1)
		par = oshape.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Parameter.value").getvalue(i-1)
		sFP = sFP & "&Point" & i & "=" & point & "&Parameter"& i & "=" & par
	next
	xcoord=page.getstyleclassproperty("Trend_popup","left_position")
	ycoord=page.getstyleclassproperty("Trend_popup","top_position")
	set oSview=getobject("","Honeywell.Workspace.Client")
	If oSview.IsManagerActive = false then
		if xcoord="default" or ycoord="default" then
			hdxpopupbehavior.createpopupwindow sFP,0,0,1
		else 
			set oChrt_Popup=HdxPopupBehavior.FindPopup("dummy")
			if typename(oChrt_Popup)<>"Nothing" then oChrt_Popup.close
			hdxpopupbehavior.createpopupwindow sFP,xcoord,ycoord,0
		end if
    else
		hdxpopupbehavior.createpopupwindow sFP,0,0,1
	end if
end sub

sub fire_gas_pageload(oSource)

   		oSource.disabled=true

	end if

' subroutines added in 2009
sub Toggle_SCM_NameDesc (pointtype)
	on error resume next
	dim oshape, custgrp, sDesc
	set oshape = document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	set oText = oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq")
	select case pointtype
		case "cda"  sDesc = "desc"
		case "hpm", "hg"  sDesc = "ptdesc"
		case "scd" sDesc = "description"
	end select
	if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_BlockName")<>"" then
		sName = oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_BlockName")
		sDVDesc = "tagname." & sName & "."  & sDesc
	else
		if typename(oShape.objects("ConAlarmState"))<>"Nothing" then
			sName=oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").datavalue("tagname.name")
		elseif oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").dataexists("tagname.name") then
			sName=oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").datavalue("tagname.name")
	    else 
			sName = oShape.getcustomproperty("Point","Tagname")
		end if
		sDVDesc = "tagname." & sDesc
	end if
	if oText.innertext = sName then
		oText.innertext = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").datavalue(sDVDesc)
	else
		oText.innertext =sName
	end if
	if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "Toggle_SCM_NameDesc ->" & oShape.ID & " " & err.description 
end sub

sub TagnameYoking_load(oSource)
	dim oShape,Database,sDB,sTagname,sql,rs
	on error resume next
	set oshape = document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	sDB = page.GetStyleClassProperty("DatabaseFile","Filename")

	set Database = CreateObject("ADODB.Connection")
	Database.Open("DRIVER={Microsoft Access Driver (*.mdb)}; DBQ=" _
	& sDB )
	if typename(oShape.objects("ConAlarmState"))<>"Nothing" then
		sTagname=oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").datavalue("tagname.name")
	elseif oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").dataexists("tagname.name") then
		sTagname=oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").datavalue("tagname.name")
    else 
		sTagname = oShape.getcustomproperty("Point","Tagname")
	end if	
	sql = "select * from TagnameYoking WHERE Tagname Like '" & sTagname & "'"
  set rs = database.Execute(sql)
  if rs.EOF = true then 
  		oSource.disabled = true
  else
		oSource.disabled = false
  end if
database.close
set database = nothing

if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "yoking_load ->" & oShape.ID & " " & oSource & " " & err.description 
end sub

sub TagnameYoking()
	dim oShape,Database,sDB,sTagname,sql,rs,i
	on error resume next
	i=1
	set oshape = document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	sDB = page.GetStyleClassProperty("DatabaseFile","Filename")

	set Database = CreateObject("ADODB.Connection")
	Database.Open("DRIVER={Microsoft Access Driver (*.mdb)}; DBQ=" _
	& sDB )

	if typename(oShape.objects("ConAlarmState"))<>"Nothing" then
		sTagname=oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").datavalue("tagname.name")
	elseif oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").dataexists("tagname.name") then
		sTagname=oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").datavalue("tagname.name")
    else 
		sTagname = oShape.getcustomproperty("Point","Tagname")
	end if	
	 
	sql = "select * from TagnameYoking WHERE Tagname Like '" & sTagname & "'"
  set rs = database.Execute(sql)
  if rs.EOF = true then
  		window.external.Application.messagezonetext "Yoking not configured" 
  else
		while not rs.EOF and i < 4
			if not isnull(rs.fields(1).value) then 
				window.external.Application.currentpage = rs.fields(1).value
				msgbox rs.fields(1).value
			end if
			rs.movenext
			i=i+1
		wend
  end if
database.close
set database = nothing

if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "yoking_load ->" & oShape.ID & " " & oSource & " " & err.description 
end sub

sub L4Detail_load(oSource)
	dim oShape,Database,sDB,sTagname,sql,rs
	on error resume next
	set oshape = document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	sDB = page.GetStyleClassProperty("DatabaseFile","Filename")
	set Database = CreateObject("ADODB.Connection")
	Database.Open("DRIVER={Microsoft Access Driver (*.mdb)}; DBQ=" & sDB )
	if typename(oShape.objects("ConAlarmState"))<>"Nothing" then
		sTagname=oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").datavalue("tagname.name")
	elseif oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").dataexists("tagname.name") then
		sTagname=oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").datavalue("tagname.name")
    else 
		sTagname = oShape.getcustomproperty("Point","Tagname")
	end if	
	sql = "select * from L4_Detail WHERE Tagname Like '" & sTagname & "'"
    set rs = database.Execute(sql)
    if rs.EOF = true then

    else
		oSource.disabled = false
    end if
    database.close
    set database = nothing
    if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "L4Detail_load ->" & oShape.ID & " " & oSource & " " & err.description

sub L4Detail()
	dim oShape,Database,sDB,sTagname,sql,rs, sURL
	on error resume next
	i=1
	set oshape = document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	sDB = page.GetStyleClassProperty("DatabaseFile","Filename")

	set Database = CreateObject("ADODB.Connection")
	Database.Open("DRIVER={Microsoft Access Driver (*.mdb)}; DBQ=" & sDB )

	if typename(oShape.objects("ConAlarmState"))<>"Nothing" then
		sTagname=oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").datavalue("tagname.name")
	elseif oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").dataexists("tagname.name") then
		sTagname=oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").datavalue("tagname.name")
    else 
		sTagname = oShape.getcustomproperty("Point","Tagname")
	end if	
	 
	sql = "select * from L4_Detail WHERE Tagname Like '" & sTagname & "'"
    set rs = database.Execute(sql)
    if rs.EOF = true then
  		window.external.Application.messagezonetext "L4Detail not configured" 
    else
  	if not isnull(rs.fields(3).value) then sURL = rs.fields(3).value & "?currentpoint=" & sTagname 
		while not rs.EOF 
			if not isnull(rs.fields(1).value) then sURL = sURL & "&" & rs.fields(2).value & "=" & rs.fields(1).value 
			rs.movenext
		wend
    end if
	database.close
	set database = nothing
	if sURL <>"" then window.external.Application.currentpage = sURL
	if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "yoking_load ->" & oShape.ID & " " & oSource & " " & err.description 
end sub]]></script></scripts><parameters><parameter name="Revision" type="Num" description="" defaultvalue="R400 Rev.0"/></parameters><shapes><shape><element><html><content><![CDATA[

dim tid_timeout,sAlarmGroupName, bCancel, bTrdResp

const  iQualityGood = 192
const  iQualityOffScan = 8
const  iQualityBad = 4
const  iQualityAccessNotPermitted = 1024
