'Allow only tags of type INPUT and TEXTAREA to be selected.
sub Page_OnSelectStart	
	dim InpName
	on error resume next
	InpName=window.event.SrcElement.TagName
	if InpName <> "INPUT" and InpName<>"TEXTAREA" then window.event.returnvalue = false
end sub

'Show the appropriate tooltip 
sub Page_OnMouseOver	
	dim oObj, iNrPoints,sPointNames(10)
	on error resume next
	set oobj=window.event.srcelement
	if isobject (oObj.onmouseover) then exit sub
	if len(oObj.id)<14 then exit sub
	if instr(ucase(right(oObj.id,16)),"SCTXTSELECTBOX")<>0 then 
		set oShape = oObj.parentnode.parentnode
 		oObj.style.cursor = "hand"
 		if oShape.getcustomproperty("point","tagname")<>"" then 		    
 			for n=0 to oShape.customProperties.Count -1
 			   if lcase(oShape.customProperties.Item(n).type) = "point" and instr(oShape.customProperties.Item(n).name,"Analog")=0 then 
				  iNrPoints = iNrPoints + 1
 				  sPointNames(iNrPoints) =	oshape.customProperties.Item(n).value
    		   end if
 			next
  			If iNrPoints = 1 Then
  	 			if typename(oShape.objects("ConAlarmState"))<>"Nothing" then
					sPoints=oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").datavalue("tagname.name")
				elseif oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").dataexists("tagname.name") then
					sPoints=oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").datavalue("tagname.name")
                else 
					sPoints = sPointNames(1)
				end if	
	  			status = "Analog"
				if (not oShape.objects("TxtError") is nothing) and (oShape.objects("TxtEUDesc") is nothing) or (not oShape.objects("TxtPV") is nothing) or (not oShape.objects("RecPV") is nothing) then status = "Status"
      			if (not oShape.objects("TxtError") is nothing) and (not oShape.objects("IndVal") is nothing or not oShape.objects("TxtOP")is nothing) then status = "RegValve"
                if (not oShape.objects("LinScale") is nothing) then status = "Analog"
	 			if status = "Analog" then
					PvTxt = ToolTip_PV(oShape,status)
					SpTxt = ToolTip_SP(oShape,status)
					OpTxt = ToolTip_OP(oShape)
					ModeTxt = ToolTip_Mode(oShape) 
					AlarmTxt = ToolTip_Alm(oShape,"")
					sToolTip= sPoints & vbCR & SPTxt  & PVTxt & OpTxt &  ModeTxt & AlarmTxt
					if AlarmTxt <> "" then
						oObj.title = sToolTip
					else
						oObj.title = mid(sToolTip,1,len(sToolTip)-1)
					end if
	  			elseif status = "RegValve" then
	  				PVTxt = ToolTip_PV(oShape,status)
					OPTxt = ToolTip_OP(oShape)
					AlarmTxt = ToolTip_Alm(oShape,"")
					sToolTip= sPoints & vbCR & PVTxt  & OPTxt & AlarmTxt
					if AlarmTxt <> "" then
						oObj.title = sToolTip
					else
						oObj.title = mid(sToolTip,1,len(sToolTip)-1)
					end if
	  			else
					AlarmTxt = ToolTip_Alm(oShape,"")
					sToolTip = sPoints & vbcr &  PVTxt &  AlarmTxt 
					if AlarmTxt <> "" then
						oObj.title = sToolTip
					else
						oObj.title = mid(sToolTip,1,len(sToolTip)-1)
					end if
	  			end if
  			Elseif iNrPoints > 1 Then
    			sPoints = ucase(sPointNames(1)) & vbcr
				For i=2 to iNrPoints
	  				if sPointNames(i)<>sPointNames(1) Then 
	  					sPoints = sPoints & vbcr & ucase(sPointNames(i)) 
						AlarmTxt = ToolTip_Alm(oShape, i-1)
						if AlarmTxt <> "" and typename(oShape.objects("ConAlarmState" & i-1))<>"Nothing" then
							if oShape.objects("ConAlarmState" & i-1).style.visibility = "inherit" then sAlm = AlarmTxt
						end if
       				end if
     			Next 
				if sAlm<>"" then sPoints = sPoints & vbcr & vbcr & sAlm
				oObj.title= sPoints 
  			End If
			if oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","FaceplateFile")="-" then 
				oObj.style.cursor="default"
			else
				oObj.style.cursor="hand"
			end if
			if oShape.getcustomproperty("text","faceplatefile")="-" then oShape.objects("sctxtselectbox").style.cursor="default"
   		end if
 	elseif ucase(right(oObj.id,14))="SCTXTSELECTION" then 
    	set oShape=oObj.parentnode.parentnode
		oShape.objects("ScTxtSelection").style.cursor="hand"
 	end if
end sub

'Get the PV Text shown in tooltip. Called within Page_OnMouseOver
function ToolTip_PV (oShape,PointType)
	on error resume next
	select case PointType
		case "Analog"
			sPV = oShape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_pv")
			sPos = instr(sPV,".")
			if spos>0 then sPV = mid(sPV,sPos+1,len(sPv)-sPos)
    		EUDesc = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").Datavalue("tagname.cp_eudesc")
			PVFormat = oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_pvformat")
			if PVFormat="" then PVFormat = 2
			PVFormat = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").Datavalue("tagname.cp_pvformat")
			PV = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").value
			If isnumeric(PV) and sPV<>"" Then 
				PVTxt = ucase(sPV) & "="  & FormatNumber (PV, PVFormat) & " " & EuDesc & vbcr
			elseif sPV<>"" then
				PVTxt =ucase(sPV) & "="  & PV & vbcr
			end if	
		case "RegValve"
			sPV = oShape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_pv")
			sPos = instr(sPV,".")
			if spos>0 then sPV = mid(sPV,sPos+1,len(sPv)-sPos)
	    	PV = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").value
	    	If sPV<>"" Then PVTxt = ucase(sPV) & "="  & FormatNumber (PV, 0) & " " & "%" & vbcr			
	end select
	ToolTip_PV= PVTxt
end function

'Get the SP Text shown in tooltip. Called within Page_OnMouseOver
function ToolTip_SP (oShape,PointType)
	on error resume next
	select case PointType
		case "Analog"
			sSP = oShape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_sp")
			sPos = instr(sSP,".")
			if spos>0 then sSP = mid(sSP,sPos+1,len(sSP)-sPos)
			if sSP = "" and oShape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_dev") <>"" then sSP = "SP"		
			SP=""
			SP = oShape.objects("ConAlpSP").value
			SP = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").datavalue("Tagname.cp_sp")
			SP = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").value - oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").Datavalue("tagname.cp_dev")
			PVFormat = oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_pvformat")
			if PVFormat="" then PVFormat = 2
			PVFormat = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").Datavalue("tagname.cp_pvformat")
			EUDesc = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").Datavalue("tagname.cp_eudesc")		
			If isnumeric(SP) Then SPTxt = vblf & ucase(sSP) & "="  & FormatNumber (SP, PVFormat) & " " & EuDesc & vbcr
	end select
	ToolTip_SP= SPTxt
end function

'Get the OP Text shown in tooltip. Called within Page_OnMouseOver
function ToolTip_OP(oShape)',PointType)
	on error resume next
			sOP = oShape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_op")
			sPos = instr(sOP,".")
			if spos>0 then sOP = mid(sOP,sPos+1,len(sOP)-sPos)
        	PVFormat=1
			OP=""
			OP = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").value
			OP = oShape.objects("ConAlpOP").value
			OP = oShape.objects("ConIndOP").value
			OP = oShape.objects("ScConAlpOP").datavalue("Tagname.cp_op")
	    	If sOP<>"" Then OPTxt = ucase(sOP) & "="  & FormatNumber (OP, PVFormat) & " " & "%" & vbcr
	ToolTip_OP = OPTxt
end function

'Get the Mode Text shown in tooltip. Called within Page_OnMouseOver
function ToolTip_Mode(oShape)
	on error resume next
	ModeTxt = oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").Datavalue("tagname.cp_mode")
	if len(ModeTxt)>0 Then ToolTip_Mode = "Mode= " & ModeTxt &vbcr
end function

'Get the Alarm Text shown in tooltip. Called within Page_OnMouseOver
function ToolTip_Alm (oShape,index)
	on error resume next
	if typename(oShape.objects("ConAlarmState" & index))<> "Nothing" then 
		AlarmTxt = oShape.objects("ConAlarmState" & index).title
		IF len(AlarmTxt)<>0 and instr(AlarmTxt,"Quality")=0 Then 
			ToolTip_Alm = "Alarm= " & AlarmTxt
		elseif index="" then 
			ToolTip_Alm = "No Alarm "
		else
			 ToolTip_Alm=""
		end if
	end if
end function

'Focus ConAlarmState if an alarm is present or ScConAlpPV when there is no alarm.
sub ObjSel
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
	set oShape=document.getelementbyID(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	set oAlarmIcon = nothing
  	for each oAlarmState in oShape.children(0).all
    	if instr(oAlarmState.classname, "alarmstate")>0 and (oAlarmState.Style.Visibility = "inherit" or oAlarmState.Style.Visibility = "")  then
			if instr(oAlarmState.title,"Unack") or instr(ucase(page.getattribute("lastselection")),"ALM_SCTXTSELECTBOX")>0 and oAlarmState.title<>"" then
				set oAlarmIcon = oAlarmState	
			end if						
    	end if
  	next
  	if oAlarmIcon is nothing Then
		for each oAlphaNum in oShape.all.tags("TEXTAREA")
			if (oAlphaNum.alarm and &H400)=&H400 then 
				set oAlarmIcon=oAlphaNum
				exit for
			end if	             
  		next
    end if 
  	if oAlarmIcon is nothing Then
    	oshape.objects("ScConAlpPV").focus
  	else
    	oAlarmIcon.focus
  	end if
end sub

'Provide Left Click behavior. Invoke Faceplate
sub All_OnClick(oSource) 
	dim oShape,soldsel
  	on error resume next
	if bTrdResp=true then bCancel=true	' Check to prevent message zone flickering, when All_TrdSel_lib_h_01_R300.sha is not present in the current display.
										' See Trend_Sup_GSH.sha trdresp subroutine
 	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	call SelectBox_Color(oSource)
    set otarget=oshape.objects("ScConAlpPV")
    sfaceplatefile=oshape.getcustomproperty("text","faceplatefile")
    if sfaceplatefile="" then
   	   set oSource.onfocus = nothing
       otarget.click 
	   oTarget.style.color = oTarget.textcolor
    elseif sfaceplatefile="-" then
       'if not isobject(oSource.onfocus) then set oSource.onfocus=getref("ObjSel")
       'oSource.focus
	   call ObjSel
    else
       scurrentpoint=oshape.getcustomproperty("point","tagname")
       sPoints= "?CurrentPoint=" & sCurrentPoint 
       for n=1 to oshape.customProperties.Count -1
  	      if oshape.customProperties.Item(n).type = "Point" or oshape.customProperties.Item(n).type = "Parameter" then 
    	    sPoints = sPoints & "&" & oshape.customProperties.Item(n).name & "=" & oshape.customProperties.Item(n).value
	      end if
       next	   
       sfaceplatefile = mid(sfaceplatefile,1,instr(1,sfaceplatefile,".htm")+3)
	   if oTarget.quality = 0  then 
	   		if ucase(Left(oShape.customproperties.Item(1).Name,3))="TAG" then
				Quality = oTarget.DataQuality(oShape.customproperties.Item(1).Name & ".cp_Tag1_pv" ) ' Used for F&G Shapes
			else
				Quality = oTarget.DataQuality(oShape.customproperties.Item(1).Name & ".cp_AlmPar1" ) ' Used for Multialarm shapes
			end if
	   else
	   		Quality = oTarget.quality
	   end if

       if (Quality and iQualityAccessNotPermitted) <> iQualityAccessNotPermitted then call hdxpopupbehavior.createpopupwindow(sfaceplatefile & spoints,0,0,1)
    end if 
    iYoke=ucase(page.getstyleclassproperty("Enable_Yoking","key"))
	if iYoke="ON" and not isnull(page.getattribute("GSH_Yoking")) then call DspYoking(oSource) 
end sub

sub All_MultAlm_OnClick(oSource) 
	dim oShape,soldsel
  	on error resume next
 	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	call SelectBox_Color(oSource)
  	for each oAlarmState in oShape.children(0).all
    	if instr(oAlarmState.classname, "alarmstate")>0 and oAlarmState.Style.Visibility = "inherit" then
			set oTarget = oAlarmState
    	end if
  	next
    sfaceplatefile=oshape.getcustomproperty("text","faceplatefile" & right(otarget.id,1))
    if sfaceplatefile="" then
   	   set oSource.onfocus = nothing
       otarget.click 
    elseif sfaceplatefile="-" then
       'if not isobject(oSource.onfocus) then set oSource.onfocus=getref("ObjSel")
       'oSource.focus
	   call ObjSel
    else
       scurrentpoint=oshape.getcustomproperty("point","tagname")
       sPoints= "?CurrentPoint=" & sCurrentPoint 
       for n=1 to oshape.customProperties.Count -1
  	      if oshape.customProperties.Item(n).type = "Point" or oshape.customProperties.Item(n).type = "Parameter" then 
    	    sPoints = sPoints & "&" & oshape.customProperties.Item(n).name & "=" & oshape.customProperties.Item(n).value
	      end if
       next	   
       sfaceplatefile = mid(sfaceplatefile,1,instr(1,sfaceplatefile,".htm")+3)
       if (Quality and iQualityAccessNotPermitted) <> iQualityAccessNotPermitted then call hdxpopupbehavior.createpopupwindow(sfaceplatefile & spoints,0,0,1)
    end if 
end sub

sub All_MultAlm_OnCMenu(oSource) 
	dim oShape,soldsel
  	on error resume next
 	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	call SelectBox_Color(oSource)
  	for each oAlarmState in oShape.children(0).all
    	if instr(oAlarmState.classname, "alarmstate")>0 and oAlarmState.Style.Visibility = "inherit" then
			set oTarget = oAlarmState
    	end if
  	next
   	set oSource.onfocus = nothing    
    sCMfile=oshape.getcustomproperty("text","ContextMenuFile" & right(otarget.id,1))
    if isnull(page.getattribute("CMenu_GSH")) then
		use_cm_shape=false
	else 
		use_cm_shape = true
	end if
	'if not isobject(oSource.onfocus) then set oSource.onfocus=getref("ObjSel")
    if sCMfile="-" then
      'oSource.focus
    elseif sCMfile="" or use_cm_shape = false then
      oTarget.fireevent("oncontextmenu")
      'oSource.focus
    else
      hdxpopupbehavior.invokeshortcutmenu sCMfile,0,0
      'oSource.focus    
    end if
	call ObjSel
end sub

'Provide Right Click behavior. Invoke Context Menu.
sub All_OnCMenu(oSource)
    dim oShape,soldsel
    on error resume next
    if bTrdResp=true then bCancel=true	' Check to prevent message zone flickering, when All_TrdSel_lib_h_01_R300.sha is not present in the current display.
										' See Trend_Sup_GSH.sha trdresp subroutine
    set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
    call SelectBox_Color(oSource)
    'if not isobject(oSource.onactivate) then set oSource.onactivate=getref("ObjSel")
    sCMfile=oshape.getcustomproperty("text","ContextMenuFile")
    if isnull(page.getattribute("CMenu_GSH")) then
		use_cm_shape=false
	else 
		use_cm_shape = true
	end if
    if sCMfile="-" then
      'oSource.focus
    elseif sCMfile="" or use_cm_shape = false then
	  'oSource.focus 	
      oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").fireevent("oncontextmenu")      
    else
      'oSource.focus
      hdxpopupbehavior.invokeshortcutmenu sCMfile,0,0    
    end if
	call objSel
end sub

'Change the ScTxtSelectBox color when selected
sub SelectBox_Color(oSource)
	dim soldsel,skey,bYok, status, oShape 
	on error resume next
	soldsel=page.getattribute("lastselection")
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	if not isnull(soldsel) then page.all.item(soldsel).styleclass = "ScTxtSelectBox_NotSelected"
    page.setattribute "lastselection",osource.id
	if ucase(right(oSource.id,18)) <> "ALM_SCTXTSELECTBOX" then osource.styleclass = "ScTxtSelectBox_Selected"
	skey=page.getstyleclassproperty("Enable_Assoc_Focus","key")
	if ucase(skey)="ON" and not isnull(page.getattribute("GSH_Yoking")) then
	    call assoc_focus(oSource,soldsel)
    if not isnull(soldsel) then
		    set oShape_Old=document.getelementbyid(soldsel).parentnode.parentnode
			status="Analog"
			if (not oShape_old.objects("TxtError") is nothing) and (oShape_old.objects("TxtEUDesc") is nothing) or (not oShape_old.objects("TxtPV") is nothing) or (not oShape_old.objects("RecPV") is nothing) then status = "Status"
	    	if (not oShape_old.objects("TxtError") is nothing) and (not oShape_old.objects("IndVal") is nothing or not oShape_old.objects("TxtOP")is nothing) then status = "RegValve"
	    	if (not oShape_old.objects("LinScale") is nothing) then status = "Analog"  		  
			select case status
				case "Analog" if oShape_old.Objects("RecFrame").styleclass="RecFrameInPrincipalFocus" then oShape_old.Objects("RecFrame").styleclass="RecFrame"
				case "Status", "RegValve" if oShape_old.Objects("RecFrame").styleclass="RecFrameInPrincipalFocus" then oShape_old.Objects("RecFrame").styleclass="RecFrameNoInFocus"
	        end select	
		end if 
		oShape.Objects("RecFrame").styleclass="RecFrameInPrincipalFocus"
	end if
	if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " SelectBox_Color ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'Determine the most importatnt alarm. Used in shapes with multiple tagnames
Sub Multiple_Alarm_Icon(oSource)
	Dim sMostImportantAlarm, sAlarmState, iThisImportancy, iMostImportant, oAlarmState, iAlarmCount, iInActiveAlarmCount, oAlm
	On error resume next
	iMostImportant=0
	for each oAlarmState in oSource.parentnode.children
 		if instr(oAlarmState.classname, "alarmstate")>0 then
 			iAlarmCount = iAlarmCount + 1
    		iThisImportancy = 0
    		iThisImportancy = 24+oAlarmState.Acknowledged*16 + oAlarmState.Priority*2 + oAlarmState.RTN*8
    		if iThisImportancy  = 16 then iThisImportancy = 8
    		if iThisImportancy >iMostImportant then 
       			sMostImportantAlarm = oAlarmState.id
       			iMostImportant = iThisImportancy
    		end if
			if iThisImportancy = 0 then iInActiveAlarmCount = iInActiveAlarmCount + 1 
 		end if
	next
	for each oAlarmState in oSource.parentnode.children
   		if instr(oAlarmState.classname, "alarmstate")>0 then 
      		if oAlarmState.id = sMostImportantAlarm or iAlarmCount = iInActiveAlarmCount then
 	  	 		oAlarmState.Style.Visibility = "inherit"
				set oAlm = oAlarmState
      		else
         		oAlarmState.Style.Visibility = "hidden"
      		end if
    	end if
	next
 call All_ObjAlm_OnUpdate(oAlm)
 if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " Multiple_Alarm_Icon ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
End Sub

' -------------------------------------
' Navigations 
' -------------------------------------

sub Nav_SelectBox_OnClick(oSource)
	on error resume next
	window.external.currentpage = oSource.parentnode.parentnode.getcustomproperty("Text","DspName")
end sub

sub Nav_Alarm_OnDataChange(oSource,oTextLow,oTextHigh,oTextUrg,oSymbLow,oSymbHigh,oSymbUrg)
	dim oShape,iTotalLowAlarms,iTotalLowUnackAlarms,iTotalHighAlarms,iTotalHighUnackAlarms,iTotalUrgentAlarms,iTotalUrgentUnackAlarms,iTotalLowRTN,iTotalHighRTN,iTotalUrgentRTN
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	iTotalLowAlarms=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalLowAlarms")
	iTotalLowUnackAlarms=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalLowUnackAlarms")
	iTotalHighAlarms=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalHighAlarms")
	iTotalHighUnackAlarms=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalHighUnackAlarms")
	iTotalUrgentAlarms=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalUrgentAlarms")
	iTotalUrgentUnackAlarms=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalUrgentUnackAlarms")
	iTotalLowRTN = oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalLowRTNUnackAlarms")
	iTotalHighRTN = oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalHighRTNUnackAlarms")
	iTotalUrgentRTN = oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalUrgentRTNUnackAlarms")
	if oSource.DataExists("Tagname.TotalShelvedAlarms") then iTotalShelvedAlarms=oSource.DataValue("Tagname.TotalShelvedAlarms")
	if typename(oShape.objects("OvlShelved"))<> "Nothing" then set oShlvdSymb=oShape.objects("OvlShelved")
	if typename(oShape.objects("TxtShelved"))<> "Nothing" then set oShlvdTxt=oShape.objects("TxtShelved")
	if iTotalShelvedAlarms>0  then
		if typename(oShape.objects("OvlShelved"))<>"Nothing" then oShlvdSymb.style.visibility="inherit"
		if typename(oShape.objects("TxtShelved"))<> "Nothing" then oShlvdTxt.style.visibility="inherit"
		if typename(oShape.objects("TxtShelved"))<> "Nothing" then oShlvdTxt.value=iTotalShelvedAlarms
    else
		if typename(oShape.objects("OvlShelved"))<>"Nothing" then oShlvdSymb.style.visibility="hidden"
		if typename(oShape.objects("TxtShelved"))<> "Nothing" then oShlvdTxt.style.visibility="hidden"
	end if
	if iTotalLowAlarms>0 then
		oSymbLow.style.visibility = "inherit"
   		oSymbLow.fillcolor = AlarmStateIconFactory.LowColor
		oSymbLow.linecolor = AlarmStateIconFactory.LowColor
    	if not isnull(oTextLow) then
    		oTextLow.style.visibility="inherit"	
			oTextLow.innertext=iTotalLowAlarms
    	end if
		if iTotalLowUnackAlarms>0 and iTotalLowUnackAlarms = iTotalLowRTN then
			oSymbLow.fillcolor = "transparent"
			oSymbLow.linecolorblink=true
			oSymbLow.fillcolorblink=false
  		elseif iTotalLowUnackAlarms>0 then
			oSymbLow.fillcolorblink=true
			oSymbLow.linecolorblink=false
		else
			oSymbLow.fillcolorblink=false
    		oSymbLow.linecolorblink=false
  		end if
	else
  		oSymbLow.style.visibility="hidden"
    	if not isnull(oTextLow) then oTextLow.Style.visibility="hidden"
	end if
	if iTotalHighAlarms>0 then
		oSymbHigh.style.visibility = "inherit"
	   	oSymbHigh.fillcolor = AlarmStateIconFactory.HighColor
		oSymbHigh.linecolor = AlarmStateIconFactory.HighColor
    	if not isnull(oTextHigh) then
    		oTextHigh.style.visibility="inherit"	
			oTextHigh.innertext=iTotalHighAlarms
    	end if
		if iTotalHighUnackAlarms>0 and iTotalHighUnackAlarms = iTotalHighRTN then
			oSymbHigh.fillcolor = "transparent"
			oSymbHigh.linecolorblink=true
			oSymbHigh.fillcolorblink=false
  		elseif iTotalHighUnackAlarms>0 then
			oSymbHigh.fillcolorblink=true
			oSymbHigh.linecolorblink=false
		else
			oSymbHigh.fillcolorblink=false
    		oSymbHigh.linecolorblink=false
  		end if
	else
  		oSymbHigh.style.visibility="hidden"
    	if not isnull(oTextHigh) then oTextHigh.Style.visibility="hidden"
	end if
	if iTotalUrgentAlarms>0 then
		oSymbUrg.style.visibility = "inherit"
		oSymbUrg.fillcolor = AlarmStateIconFactory.UrgentColor
		oSymbUrg.linecolor = AlarmStateIconFactory.UrgentColor
    	if not isnull(oTextUrg) then
    		oTextUrg.style.visibility="inherit"	
			oTextUrg.innertext=iTotalUrgentAlarms
    	end if
		if iTotalUrgentUnackAlarms>0 and iTotalUrgentUnackAlarms = iTotalUrgentRTN then
			oSymbUrg.fillcolor = "transparent"
			oSymbUrg.linecolorblink=true
			oSymbUrg.fillcolorblink=false
  		elseif iTotalUrgentUnackAlarms>0 then
			oSymbUrg.fillcolorblink=true
			oSymbUrg.linecolorblink=false
		else
			oSymbUrg.fillcolorblink=false
    		oSymbUrg.linecolorblink=false
  		end if
	else
  		oSymbUrg.style.visibility="hidden"
    	if not isnull(oTextUrgent) then oTextUrg.Style.visibility="hidden"
	end if
	if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " Nav_Alarm_OnDataChange ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub Nav_Alarm3Icons_OnDataChange (oSource)
	dim oShape,iTotalLowAlarms,iTotalLowUnackAlarms,iTotalHighAlarms,iTotalHighUnackAlarms,iTotalUrgentAlarms,iTotalUrgentUnackAlarms,iTotalLowRTN,iTotalHighRTN,iTotalUrgentRTN
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	iTotalLowAlarms=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalLowAlarms")
	iTotalLowUnackAlarms=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalLowUnackAlarms")
	iTotalHighAlarms=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalHighAlarms")
	iTotalHighUnackAlarms=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalHighUnackAlarms")
	iTotalUrgentAlarms=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalUrgentAlarms")
	iTotalUrgentUnackAlarms=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalUrgentUnackAlarms")
	iTotalLowRTN = oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalLowRTNUnackAlarms")
	iTotalHighRTN = oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalHighRTNUnackAlarms")
	iTotalUrgentRTN = oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalUrgentRTNUnackAlarms")
	if oSource.dataexists("Tagname.TotalShelvedAlarms") then
		set oShlvdSymb=oShape.objects("AlmShelved")
		if oSource.DataValue("Tagname.TotalShelvedAlarms")>0 then 
			oShlvdSymb.priority=0
			oShlvdSymb.shelved=true
			oShlvdSymb.style.visibility="inherit"
        else 
			oShlvdSymb.style.visibility="hidden"
		end if
	end if
	if iTotalLowAlarms>0 then
   		oShape.Objects("AlmLow").priority=1
    	if iTotalLowUnackAlarms>0 and iTotalLowUnackAlarms = iTotalLowRTN then
			oShape.Objects("AlmLow").RTN = true
			oShape.Objects("AlmLow").acknowledged=false
		elseif iTotalLowUnackAlarms>0 then
    		oShape.Objects("AlmLow").acknowledged=false
			oShape.Objects("AlmLow").RTN = false
  		else
    		oShape.Objects("AlmLow").acknowledged=true
			oShape.Objects("AlmLow").RTN = false
  		end if
	else
   		oShape.Objects("AlmLow").priority=0
		oShape.Objects("AlmLow").acknowledged=false
		oShape.Objects("AlmLow").RTN = false
	end if
	if iTotalHighAlarms>0 then
   		oShape.Objects("AlmHigh").priority=2
    	if iTotalHighUnackAlarms>0 and iTotalHighUnackAlarms = iTotalHighRTN then
			oShape.Objects("AlmHigh").RTN = true
			oShape.Objects("AlmHigh").acknowledged=false
		elseif iTotalHighUnackAlarms>0 then
    		oShape.Objects("AlmHigh").acknowledged=false
			oShape.Objects("AlmHigh").RTN = false
  		else
    		oShape.Objects("AlmHigh").acknowledged=true
			oShape.Objects("AlmHigh").RTN = false
  		end if
	else
   		oShape.Objects("AlmHigh").priority=0
		oShape.Objects("AlmHigh").acknowledged=false
		oShape.Objects("AlmHigh").RTN = false
	end if
	if iTotalUrgentAlarms>0 then
   		oShape.Objects("AlmUrgent").priority=3
    	if iTotalUrgentUnackAlarms>0 and iTotalUrgentUnackAlarms = iTotalUrgentRTN then
			oShape.Objects("AlmUrgent").RTN = true
			oShape.Objects("AlmUrgent").acknowledged=false
		elseif iTotalUrgentUnackAlarms>0 then
    		oShape.Objects("AlmUrgent").acknowledged=false
			oShape.Objects("AlmUrgent").RTN = false
  		else
    		oShape.Objects("AlmUrgent").acknowledged=true
			oShape.Objects("AlmUrgent").RTN = false
  		end if
	else
   		oShape.Objects("AlmUrgent").priority=0
		oShape.Objects("AlmUrgent").acknowledged=false
		oShape.Objects("AlmUrgent").RTN = false
	end if
	if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " Nav_Alarm3Icons_OnDataChange ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

Sub Navs_HighPriority_Alarm_OnDataChange(oSource)
	dim oShape,iULow,iUHigh,iUUrgent,iALow,iAHigh,iAUrgent
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	iUUrgent=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalUrgentUnackAlarms")
	iUHigh=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalHighUnackAlarms")
	iULow=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalLowUnackAlarms")
	iAUrgent=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalUrgentAlarms")
	iAHigh=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalHighAlarms")
	iALow=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalLowAlarms")
	iRTNUrgent = oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalUrgentRTNUnackAlarms")
	iRTNHigh = oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalHighRTNUnackAlarms")
	iRTNLow = oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalLowRTNUnackAlarms")
	if iUUrgent>0 and iUUrgent>iRTNUrgent then
 	  	oShape.Objects("Alarm").priority=3
		oShape.Objects("Alarm").RTN=false
		oShape.Objects("Alarm").acknowledged=false
	elseif iUHigh>0 and iUHigh>iRTNHigh then
 	    oShape.Objects("Alarm").priority=2
		oShape.Objects("Alarm").RTN=false
		oShape.Objects("Alarm").acknowledged=false
	elseif iULow>0 and iULow>iRTNLow then
 	    oShape.Objects("Alarm").priority=1
		oShape.Objects("Alarm").RTN=false
		oShape.Objects("Alarm").acknowledged=false
	elseif iRTNUrgent>0 then
		oShape.Objects("Alarm").priority=3
		oShape.Objects("Alarm").RTN=true
		oShape.Objects("Alarm").acknowledged=false
	elseif iRTNHigh>0 then
		oShape.Objects("Alarm").priority=2
		oShape.Objects("Alarm").RTN=true
		oShape.Objects("Alarm").acknowledged=false
	elseif iRTNLow>0 then
		oShape.Objects("Alarm").priority=1
		oShape.Objects("Alarm").RTN=true
		oShape.Objects("Alarm").acknowledged=false
	elseif iAUrgent>0 then
  	  	oShape.Objects("Alarm").priority=3
	  	oShape.Objects("Alarm").RTN=false
		oShape.Objects("Alarm").acknowledged=true
	elseif iAHigh>0 then
 	   	oShape.Objects("Alarm").priority=2
		oShape.Objects("Alarm").RTN=false
		oShape.Objects("Alarm").acknowledged=true
	elseif iALow>0 then
 	   	oShape.Objects("Alarm").priority=1
	   	oShape.Objects("Alarm").RTN=false
		oShape.Objects("Alarm").acknowledged=true
	else
 	   oShape.Objects("Alarm").priority=0
	   oShape.Objects("Alarm").RTN=false
	   oShape.Objects("Alarm").acknowledged=true
	end if
	if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " Navs_HighPriority_Alarm_OnDataChange ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

Sub Navs_HighPriority_FillClr_Alarm_OnDataChange(oSource)
	dim oShape,iULow,iUHigh,iUUrgent,iALow,iAHigh,iAUrgent
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	iUUrgent=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalUrgentUnackAlarms")
	iUHigh=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalHighUnackAlarms")
	iULow=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalLowUnackAlarms")
	iAUrgent=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalUrgentAlarms")
	iAHigh=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalHighAlarms")
	iALow=oSource.dataValue("Tagname.TotalLowAlarms")
	if iUUrgent>0 then
  	  	oShape.Objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolor=AlarmStateIconFactory.UrgentColor
		oShape.Objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolorblink=true
	elseif iUHigh>0 then
    	oShape.Objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolor=AlarmStateIconFactory.HighColor
		oShape.Objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolorblink=true
	elseif iULow>0 then
    	oShape.Objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolor=AlarmStateIconFactory.LowColor
		oShape.Objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolorblink=true
	elseif iAUrgent>0 then
    	oShape.Objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolor=AlarmStateIconFactory.UrgentColor
		oShape.Objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolorblink=false
	elseif iAHigh>0 then
    	oShape.Objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolor=AlarmStateIconFactory.HighColor
		oShape.Objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolorblink=false
	elseif iALow>0 then
    	oShape.Objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolor=AlarmStateIconFactory.LowColor
		oShape.Objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolorblink=false
	else
		if typename(oShape.Objects("InferiorBorderLin1"))<>"Nothing" then
			oShape.Objects("ScPlgAlarm").styleclass = "NavInnerObject"
		else
			oShape.Objects("ScPlgAlarm").styleclass = "NavFrame"
		end if
		oShape.Objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolorblink=false
	end if
	if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " Navs_HighPriority_FillClr_Alarm_OnDataChange ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

Sub NavsArrow_HP_AlpAlarm_OnDataChange(oSource)
	dim oShape,iUnack,iAck
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	oShape.Objects("HprLnkDsp").style.color=page.GetStyleClassProperty("TagNameEUDesc", "hw-text-color")
	iUnack=oSource.dataValue("tagname.TotalUnackAlarms")
	iAck=oSource.dataValue("tagname.TotalAckAlarms")
	if (iAck>0) or (iUnack>0) then
    	oShape.objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolor=vbred
		if iUnack > 0 then
	  	  	oShape.objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolorblink=true
		else
   			oShape.objects("ScPlgAlarm").fillcolorblink=false
		end if
	else
    	oShape.Objects("ScPlgAlarm").styleclass = "NavFrame"
	end if
	if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " NavsArrow_HP_AlpAlarm_OnDataChange ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

end sub

sub All_DigState_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   set oPV=oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV")
  	iPV=oSource.breakpointvalueenum
   	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   	oPV.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & index		
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " All_DigState_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description    
end sub

sub All_DigStateCText_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape,oPVObj
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   set oPVObj=oShape.objects("TxtPV")
   if (oSource.quality and iQualityBad)=iQualityBad and  (oSource.quality and iQualityOffScan)<>iQualityOffScan then
      oPVObj.innertext="--------" 
      oPVObj.textcolor=vbRed
   else
	  oPVObj.innertext=oShape.GetCustomProperty("Text","cp_TextForPV" &  cstr(oSource.value))
      oPVObj.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForPV" & cstr(oSource.value))
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " All_DigStateCText_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

'-------------------------------------
'Interlock and Bypass
'-------------------------------------
sub All_ILBPGraph_AlpError_OnUpdate(oSource)
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
    set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	if oShape.getcustomproperty("Point","Tag2_Inhibited")<>"" then 
		iQuality2 =(oSource.dataquality("Tag2_Inhibited.cp_Tag2_pv") and iQualityGood)
	else
		iQuality2 = iQualityGood
	end if
    if ((oSource.quality and iQualityGood)=iQualityGood) and (iQuality2=iQualityGood) then
       oShape.objects("TxtError").styleclass="TxtError_NoError"
    else
       oShape.objects("TxtError").styleclass="TxtError_InError"
    end if
    if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " Scd_ILBPGraph_AlpError_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description    
end sub

sub All_ILBP_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource,param)
	dim oShape, iPV
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	if param<=2 then
	  iValueONInt = cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_PlgInterlock_ONValue"))
	  if iValueONInt=cint(oSource.value) then : iInt=1 : else : iInt=0 : end if
	end if
	if param=2 then
	  iValueONByp = cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_TxtBypass_ONValue"))
      if iValueONByp=cint(oSource.datavalue("Tag2_Inhibited.cp_Tag2_pv")) then : iByp=1 : else : iByp=0 : end if
    end if
    if param=2 then 
	  iPV=2*iInt+iByp
    elseif param=1 then
	  iPV=iInt 
	end if
	if param=3 then iPV="ForPV" & cint(oSource.value)    
	index = oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_Style" & iPV)
    oShape.objects("PlgInterLock").styleclass = "PlgInterLock_" & index	
	if param>=2 then oShape.objects("TxtBypass").styleclass = "TxtBypass_" & index	
	if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "Scd_ILBP_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description       			
end sub

'--------------------------------------
'Alarm Object 
'--------------------------------------

sub All_ObjAlm_OnUpdate(oSource)
    dim oShape
    on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	sAlmName=oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_AlmObjName")
	if sAlmName="-" then exit sub 
	set oAlmIcon=oSource
	iPriority=oAlmIcon.priority
	bUnAcknowledged=not oAlmIcon.acknowledged
    bRTN=oAlmIcon.RTN
	set oAlmObj=oShape.objects(sAlmName)
	sAlmObjAlmStyleUnack=sAlmName & "_Alarm_Unack"
	sAlmObjAlmStyleAck=sAlmName & "_Alarm_Ack"
	sAlmObjNoAlmStyle=sAlmName & "_NoAlarm"
	oAlmObj.styleclass = sAlmObjNoAlmStyle
    sAlmObjProp=oAlmObj.GetStyleProperty("hw-Alarm-Property")	
    select case iPriority
		case 0:
			oAlmObj.styleclass=sAlmObjNoAlmStyle
        case 1:			
            sAlmObjPropColor=oAlmObj.id & "." & sAlmObjProp & "=" & """" & AlarmStateIconfactory.lowcolor & """"
			execute(sAlmObjPropColor)
        case 2:
            sAlmObjPropColor=oAlmObj.id & "." & sAlmObjProp & "=" & """" & AlarmStateIconfactory.highcolor  & """"
			execute(sAlmObjPropColor)
        case 3:
			sAlmObjPropColor=oAlmObj.id & "." & sAlmObjProp & "=" & """" & AlarmStateIconfactory.urgentcolor & """"
			execute(sAlmObjPropColor)
		case else:	
			oAlmObj.styleclass=sAlmObjNoAlmStyle			    
	end select
	if bUnAcknowledged then
		oAlmObj.styleclass=sAlmObjAlmStyleUnack
	else		
		oAlmObj.styleclass=sAlmObjAlmStyleAck
	end if
    if bRTN then oAlmObj.styleclass=sAlmObjNoAlmStyle
	if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "All_ObjAlm_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub


'--------------------------------------
'Alarm Object 
'--------------------------------------
'Provide Left Click behavior for radar objects. Invoke Faceplate
sub All_Radar_OnClick(oSource)
	dim oShape,soldsel, index, Quality
  	on error resume next
	if bTrdResp=true then bCancel=true	' Check to prevent message zone flickering, when All_TrdSel_lib_h_01_R300.sha is not present in the current display.
										' See Trend_Sup_GSH.sha trdresp subroutine
 	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	call SelectBox_Color(oSource)
	index = right(oSource.id,1)
    if typename(oshape.objects("ConAlarmState" & index))<> "Nothing" then
		set otarget=oshape.objects("ConAlarmState" & index)
	elseif  typename(oshape.objects("ScConAlpPV" & index))<> "Nothing" then
		set otarget=oshape.objects("ScConAlpPV" & index)
	end if
	sfaceplatefile=oshape.getcustomproperty("text","faceplatefile" & index)
    if sfaceplatefile="" then
   	   set oSource.onfocus = nothing
       otarget.click 
	   oTarget.style.color = oTarget.textcolor
    elseif sfaceplatefile="-" then
       'if not isobject(oSource.onfocus) then set oSource.onfocus=getref("ObjSel")
       'oSource.focus
	   call ObjSel
    else
       scurrentpoint=oshape.getcustomproperty("point","EdgeTag"& index)
       sPoints= "?CurrentPoint=" & sCurrentPoint 
       for n=1 to oshape.customProperties.Count -1
  	      if oshape.customProperties.Item(n).type = "Point" or oshape.customProperties.Item(n).type = "Parameter" then 
    	    sPoints = sPoints & "&" & oshape.customProperties.Item(n).name & "=" & oshape.customProperties.Item(n).value
	      end if
       next	   
       sfaceplatefile = mid(sfaceplatefile,1,instr(1,sfaceplatefile,".htm")+3)
	   Quality = oTarget.quality
       if (Quality and iQualityAccessNotPermitted) <> iQualityAccessNotPermitted then call hdxpopupbehavior.createpopupwindow(sfaceplatefile & spoints,0,0,1)
    end if 
end sub

'Provide Right Click behavior for radar object. Invoke Context Menu.
sub All_Radar_OnCMenu(oSource)
    dim oShape,soldsel
    on error resume next
    if bTrdResp=true then bCancel=true	' Check to prevent message zone flickering, when All_TrdSel_lib_h_01_R300.sha is not present in the current display.
										' See Trend_Sup_GSH.sha trdresp subroutine
    set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
    call SelectBox_Color(oSource)
    'if not isobject(oSource.onactivate) then set oSource.onactivate=getref("ObjSel_Radar")
	index = right(oSource.id,1)
    sCMfile=oshape.getcustomproperty("text","ContextMenuFile" & index)
    if isnull(page.getattribute("CMenu_GSH")) then
		use_cm_shape=false
	else 
		use_cm_shape = true
	end if
    if sCMfile="-" then
      'oSource.focus
    elseif sCMfile="" or use_cm_shape = false then
	  	'oSource.focus
		if typename(oshape.objects("ConAlarmState" & index))<> "Nothing" then
		    oShape.objects("ConAlarmState" & index).fireevent("oncontextmenu")      
		elseif  typename(oshape.objects("ScConAlpPV" & index))<> "Nothing" then
			oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV" & index).fireevent("oncontextmenu")
		end if
    else
      'oSource.focus
      hdxpopupbehavior.invokeshortcutmenu sCMfile,0,0    
    end if
	call ObjSel_Radar
end sub

'Focus ConAlarmState for radar objects.
sub ObjSel_Radar
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
    set oShape=document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	index=right(page.getattribute("lastselection"),1)
	if typename(oshape.objects("ConAlarmState" & index))<> "Nothing" then
	    oShape.objects("ConAlarmState" & index).focus
	elseif  typename(oshape.objects("ScConAlpPV" & index))<> "Nothing" then
	    oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV" & index).focus
	end if
end sub]]></script></scripts><parameters><parameter name="Revision" type="Num" description="" defaultvalue="R400 Rev.1"/></parameters><shapes><shape><element><html><content><![CDATA[

<?xml version="1.0"?><shapefile version="1" type="dynamic" title="" description="" useFirstShapeForBadValue="0" width="103px" height="40px"><versionheaders><versionheader name="PS" value="6.51"/></versionheaders><scripts><script event="" language="VBScript" id="17ADBAF7-D84D-4BAC-873C-008E230343FB"><![CDATA[Dim REAL01	'For CUST_CDA_IKTAR1/IKTAR2 (SootBlower graphic - SB1)
REAL01 = 0
Dim intTimer_ADVC
'-------------------------------------------------------
'Function used in shape : Multi Tags ExecState
'-------------------------------------------------------
sub CUST_HPM_ExecState_MultTags_Alp_OnUpdate(oSource)
dim oShape,PtExeCst,qual
On error resume next
set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
set oExStObj=oShape.objects("LinExecState")
stagcount = 0
for n=0 to oshape.customProperties.Count - 1
	if oshape.customProperties.Item(n).type = "Point" then 
		stagcount = stagcount + 1
	end if
next 

for i=0 to stagcount - 1
    str=cstr(i)
	if str="0" then str=""
	PtExeCst=oSource.Datavalue("Tagname" & str & ".cp_ptexecst")
	if PtExeCst=1 then
		qual=0
	else
		qual=1
		exit for
	end if
next
	if qual=0 then
	  oExStObj.styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
	else
	  oExStObj.styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
	end if
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage "CUST_HPM_ExecState_MultTags_Alp_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub
'-------------------------------------------------------
'Function used in shape : Multi Tags Graph Alp Error
'-------------------------------------------------------
sub CUST_Graph_AlpError_MultTags_OnUpdate(oSource)
dim oShape,CFGErr,Qualg,qual
On error resume next
set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
stagcount = 0
for n=0 to oshape.customProperties.Count - 1
	if oshape.customProperties.Item(n).type = "Point" then 
		stagcount = stagcount + 1
	end if
next 
qual=1
for i=0 to stagcount - 1
    str=cstr(i)
	if str="0" then str=""
	CFGErr=true
	CfgErr=oSource.qualityconfigerror("Tagname" & str & ".name")
	Qualg=false
	Qualg=oSource.qualitygood("Tagname" & str & ".name")
	if CFGErr=true or Qualg=false then qual=0
next
	if (oSource.binding.dataobjects(0).quality=33216 or oSource.binding.dataobjects(0).quality=34240) and qual=1 then
	  oShape.objects("TxtError").style.visibility="hidden"
	else
	  oShape.objects("TxtError").style.visibility="visible"
	end if
	if err.number<>0 then window.external.LogMessage "CUST_Graph_AlpError_MultTags_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'-------------------------------------------------------
'CUST_HPM_Collect_Divert_isa_h_pai_01
'-------------------------------------------------------
Sub CUST_HPM_OnOffValves_DO_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim iPV,oShape,iOP,index,indexOP,iPV1
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.value
   iPV1=oSource.datavalue("Tagname1.cp_so")
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV1)
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgIn")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index	   
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgOut")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index

   if iPV=cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_BadPVState")) then
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   else
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CUST_HPM_OnOffValves_DO_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

end sub

'-------------------------------------------------------
'CUST_K1_HLAC1
'-------------------------------------------------------
Sub CUST_K1_HLAC1_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim iPV,oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_num")
	Select Case iPV
		case 0
			oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").innertext = "OFF"
		case 1
			oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").innertext = "ON"
		case 2
			oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").innertext = "SUSP"
		case 3
			oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").innertext = "INIT"
		case 4
			oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").innertext = "ADVY"
		case else
			oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").innertext = "????"
		End Select
    if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CUST_K1_HLAC1_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description    
end sub

   end if
    if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CUST_K1_HLAC1_AlpPV_Star_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description    
end sub

'----------------------------------------------------------------
'CUST RegCtl Valves Split Range
'----------------------------------------------------------------
Sub CUST_HPM_RegCntlValve_SplitRange_AlpVal_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim rVal,iVal,rClosed,rRangeL,rRangeH,oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   rVal=oSource.datavalue("Tagname1.cp_op")
   rClosed=csng(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","ClosedValue"))
   rRangeL=csng(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","RangeLow"))
   rRangeH=csng(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","RangeHigh"))

   sRange = lcase(page.GetStyleClassProperty("UseExtended_RegValve_range","key")) 
   if rVal>100 then 
      rVal=100
   elseif rVal<0 then 
      rVal=0
   end if

   iVal = 100*(rVal - rRangeL)/(rRangeH - rRangeL)

   	if iVal>100 then 
	      iVal=100
	elseif iVal<0 then 
	      iVal=0
	end if
   if oShape.objects("TxtOP") is nothing then
      oShape.objects("IndVal").value=iVal
   else
	  oShape.objects("TxtOP").innertext=round(iVal) & "%"	
   end if

   if rVal > rClosed then
	  index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForOpen")
   else
	  index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForClose")
   end if
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgIn")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index	   
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgOut")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgOut1")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgOut1").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CUST_HPM_RegCntlValve_SplitRange_AlpVal_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

   end if

'-------------------------------------------------------
'CUST_SPLM1
'-------------------------------------------------------
Sub CUST_SPLM1_LOLimit_Onclick(oSource)
   dim oShape, MaxEntry, MinEntry, Input
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   'Trend-1 tags & ranges
	window.external.stationDataRepository.PutValue "Point_ACT_PNT", oSource.DataValue("Tagname.cp_ACT_PNT")
	MaxEntry = csng(oSource.datavalue("cp_ACT_Point.cp_SPHILM"))
	MinEntry = csng(oSource.datavalue("cp_ACT_Point.cp_PVEULO"))
	Input = InputBox(oShape.objects("ConAlpDesc").value & " LOW LIMIT", "SETPOINT LIMIT ENTRY")
	If IsNumeric(Input) and Len(Input) > 0 then
		Input = csng(Input)
		If Input >= MinEntry And Input <= MaxEntry then
			oSource.SetDataValueWithSecurityLevel "cp_ACT_Point.cp_SPLOLM", Input, 4
		ElseIf Input > MaxEntry Then
			window.external.TemporaryMessageZoneText "LIMIT OR RANGE CROSSOVER"
		Else
			window.external.TemporaryMessageZoneText "ENTRY OUT OF RANGE"
		End if
	Else
		window.external.TemporaryMessageZoneText "INVALID ENTRY"
	End if
end sub

Sub CUST_SPLM1_HILimit_Onclick(oSource)
   dim oShape, MaxEntry, MinEntry, Input
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   	window.external.stationDataRepository.PutValue "Point_ACT_PNT", oSource.DataValue("Tagname.cp_ACT_PNT")
	MaxEntry = csng(oSource.datavalue("cp_ACT_Point.cp_PVEUHI"))
	MinEntry = csng(oSource.datavalue("cp_ACT_Point.cp_SPLOLM"))
	Input = InputBox(oShape.objects("ConAlpDesc").value & " HIGH LIMIT", "SETPOINT LIMIT ENTRY")
	If IsNumeric(Input) and Len(Input) > 0 then
		Input = csng(Input)
		If Input >= MinEntry And Input <= MaxEntry then
			oSource.SetDataValueWithSecurityLevel "cp_ACT_Point.cp_SPHILM", Input, 4
		ElseIf Input > MaxEntry Then
			window.external.TemporaryMessageZoneText "LIMIT OR RANGE CROSSOVER"
		Else
			window.external.TemporaryMessageZoneText "ENTRY OUT OF RANGE"
		End if
	Else
		window.external.TemporaryMessageZoneText "INVALID ENTRY"
	End if
end sub

   	oShape.objects("TxtPVST0").innertext = oSource.datavalue("Tagname.STATETXT(0)")
	oShape.objects("TxtPVST1").innertext = oSource.datavalue("Tagname.STATETXT(1)")

	if iPV = oSource.datavalue("Tagname.STATETXT(0)") then
		oShape.objects("RecFrame0").styleclass= oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForPV0")
		oShape.objects("RecFrame1").styleclass= oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForNormal")
	elseif iPV = oSource.datavalue("Tagname.STATETXT(1)") then
		oShape.objects("RecFrame0").styleclass= oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForNormal")
		oShape.objects("RecFrame1").styleclass= oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_StyleForPV1")
	end if
    if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CUST_HPM_Refiner_PB_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description    
end sub

'-------------------------------------------------------
'CUST_CDA_IKTAR1
'-------------------------------------------------------

Sub CUST_CDA_IKTAR1_OnClick(oSource)
  dim oShape, iPV, iPV1, iPV2
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pv")
   iPV1=oSource.datavalue("tagname1.cp_pv1")
   iPV2=oSource.datavalue("tagname2.cp_pv2")
	if iPV = 99 then
		REAL01 = REAL01 + 1
		oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pv") = REAL01
		REAL01 = REAL01 + 1
		oSource.datavalue("tagname1.cp_pv1") = REAL01
		oSource.datavalue("tagname2.cp_pv2") = REAL01
	end if
end sub

Sub CUST_CDA_IKTAR1_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape, iPV
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.datavalue("tagname1.cp_pv1")
   if Not oSource.IsNaN("tagname1.cp_pv1") then
   		oSource.value = oSource.datavalue("tagname1.cp_pv1") / 2
		if iPV = 99 then
			oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").style.visibility = "hidden"
			oShape.objects("RecFrame").linecolor = vbyellow
		else
			oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").style.visibility = "visible"
			oShape.objects("RecFrame").styleclass = "RecFrame"
		end if
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CUST_CDA_IKTAR1_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description    
end sub

'-------------------------------------------------------
'CUST_K1K2_ADVC & CUST_K1K2_ADVC_InputBox
'-------------------------------------------------------
Sub CUST_K1K2_ADVC_OnClick(oSource)
   dim oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode

	'Trend-1 tags & ranges
	window.external.stationDataRepository.PutValue "Tr1_Ent", oSource.DataValue("Tagname.cp_ac_pnt")	'ADVC_ENT
	window.external.stationDataRepository.PutValue "Para_Tr1HiLm", oShape.getCustomProperty("Parameter","cp_ac_thi")
	window.external.stationDataRepository.PutValue "Para_Tr1LoLm", oShape.getCustomProperty("Parameter","cp_ac_tlo")

	'Trend-2 tags & ranges
	window.external.stationDataRepository.PutValue "Tr2_Ent1", oSource.DataValue("Tagname.cp_actsptrd")
	int103 = Fix (oSource.DataValue("Tagname.cp_ac_act"))
	window.external.stationDataRepository.PutValue "Para_ent100", "ACT_PV" & "(" & int103 & ").external"		'ACTRTENT
	window.external.stationDataRepository.PutValue "Para_Tr2HiLm", "act_thi" & "(" & int103 & ")"
	window.external.stationDataRepository.PutValue "Para_Tr2LoLm", "act_tlo" & "(" & int103 & ")"

	'Trend description
	window.external.stationDataRepository.PutValue "Para_ac_desc1", oShape.getCustomProperty("Parameter","cp_ac_desc")
	window.external.stationDataRepository.PutValue "Para_ac_desc2", "ACT_DESC" & "(" & int103 & ")"

	'Set Trend Timebase
	Timebase = oSource.DataValue("Tagname.cp_ac_ttim")
	window.external.stationDataRepository.PutValue "Para_AC_TTIM", oShape.getCustomProperty("Parameter","cp_ac_ttim")
	Select case Timebase
		case 1
			'document.getElementByID("BasicTrend1").SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,0,1,0
			'document.getElementByID("BasicTrend2").SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,0,1,0
			BasicTrend1.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,0,1,0
			BasicTrend2.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,0,1,0
			Sc_PB_Timebase.innerText = "TIMEBASE:1 MIN"
		case 2
			BasicTrend1.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,0,2,0
			BasicTrend2.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,0,2,0
			Sc_PB_Timebase.innerText = "TIMEBASE:2 MIN"
		case 3
			BasicTrend1.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,0,5,0
			BasicTrend2.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,0,5,0
			Sc_PB_Timebase.innerText = "TIMEBASE:5 MIN"
		case 4
			BasicTrend1.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,0,10,0
			BasicTrend2.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,0,10,0
			Sc_PB_Timebase.innerText = "TIMEBASE:10 MIN"
		case 5
			BasicTrend1.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,0,20,0
			BasicTrend2.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,0,20,0
			Sc_PB_Timebase.innerText = "TIMEBASE:20 MIN"
		case 6
			BasicTrend1.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,1,0,0
			BasicTrend2.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,1,0,0
			Sc_PB_Timebase.innerText = "TIMEBASE:1 HR"
		case 7
			BasicTrend1.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,2,0,0
			BasicTrend2.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,2,0,0
			Sc_PB_Timebase.innerText = "TIMEBASE:2 HR"
		case 8
			BasicTrend1.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,8,0,0
			BasicTrend2.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,8,0,0
			Sc_PB_Timebase.innerText = "TIMEBASE:8 HR"
		case 9
			BasicTrend1.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,16,0,0
			BasicTrend2.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,16,0,0
			Sc_PB_Timebase.innerText = "TIMEBASE:16 HR"
		case 10
			BasicTrend1.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,24,0,0
			BasicTrend2.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,24,0,0
			Sc_PB_Timebase.innerText = "TIMEBASE:24 HR"
		case 11
			BasicTrend1.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,48,0,0
			BasicTrend2.SetPeriod 0,0,0,0,48,0,0
			Sc_PB_Timebase.innerText = "TIMEBASE:48 HR"
	end select
end sub

Sub CUST_K1K2_ADVC_Alp_OnUpdate(oSource)
  on error resume next
	document.getElementByID("BasicTrend1").ChartArea.Plots(1).SetYAxisScale oSource.datavalue("tagname.Para_Tr1LoLm"), oSource.datavalue("tagname.Para_Tr1HiLm")
	document.getElementByID("BasicTrend1").ChartArea.Plots(2).SetYAxisScale oSource.datavalue("tagname.Para_Tr1LoLm"), oSource.datavalue("tagname.Para_Tr1HiLm")

	window.external.stationDataRepository.PutValue "Tr2_Ent2", oSource.DataValue("tagname.Para_ent100")
	document.getElementByID("BasicTrend2").ChartArea.Plots(1).SetYAxisScale oSource.datavalue("tagname.Para_Tr2LoLm"), oSource.datavalue("tagname.Para_Tr2HiLm")
	document.getElementByID("BasicTrend2").ChartArea.Plots(2).SetYAxisScale oSource.datavalue("tagname.Para_Tr2LoLm"), oSource.datavalue("tagname.Para_Tr2HiLm")
end sub

Sub CUST_K1K2_ADVC_InputBox_OnClick(oSource)
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode
	oShape.SetCustomProperty "Point", "Tr1_STRD_ENT", oSource.DataValue("Tagname.cp_ac_pnt")	'ADVC_ENT
	intTimer_ADVC = window.setInterval ("Target_Entry(" & oSource.id & ")", 300)
end sub

Function Target_Entry(oSource)
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
   	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	Desc = oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_ac_desc")
	Input = InputBox(Desc & " TARGET", "TARGET ENTRY")
	If IsNumeric(Input) and Len(Input) > 0 then
		Input = csng(Input)
		If Input <= csng(oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_ac_tgthi")) and Input >= csng(oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_ac_tgtlo")) then
			oSource.datavalue("Tr1_STRD_ENT.PVTV") = Input
		Else
			window.external.TemporaryMessageZoneText "ENTRY OUT OF RANGE"
		End if
	Else
		window.external.TemporaryMessageZoneText "INVALID ENTRY"
	End if
	window.clearInterval(intTimer_ADVC)
End Function

Sub CUST_ADVC_ACSTS_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim iPV,oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_num")
	Select Case iPV
		case 0
			oShape.objects("RecFrame2").styleclass="RecPV_Enum2"	'Yellow
			oShape.objects("ScConAlp").styleclass="A_TextSmall"		'Black
		case 1
			oShape.objects("RecFrame2").styleclass="RecPV_Enum0"	'Green
			oShape.objects("ScConAlp").styleclass="A_TextSmall"		'Black
		case 2
			oShape.objects("RecFrame2").styleclass="RecFrame"	'Gray
			oShape.objects("ScConAlp").styleclass="ScConAlpPV_Enum4"	'Magenta
		case 3
			oShape.objects("RecFrame2").styleclass="RecFrame"	'Gray
			oShape.objects("ScConAlp").styleclass="ScConAlpPV_Enum6"	'White
		case 4
			oShape.objects("RecFrame2").styleclass="RecFrame"	'Gray
			oShape.objects("ScConAlp").styleclass="ScConAlpPV_Enum0"	'Green
		case else
			oShape.objects("RecFrame2").styleclass="RecPV_Enum1"	'Red
			oShape.objects("ScConAlp").styleclass="A_TextSmall"	'Black
		End Select
    if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CUST_ADVC_ACSTS_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description    
end sub

Sub CUST_ADVC_TargetSP_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim iPV,oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_ac_spok")
   iPV1=oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_num")
   if iPV = 0 then
	   	if iPV1 = 1 then
			oShape.objects("RecFrame3").styleclass="RecPV_Enum3"	'Cyan
			oShape.objects("ConAlpTarget").styleclass="A_TextLarge"	'Black
		else
			oShape.objects("RecFrame3").styleclass="RecFrame"	'Gray
			oShape.objects("ConAlpTarget").styleclass="ScConAlpPV"	'Cyan
		end if
	else
		oShape.objects("RecFrame3").styleclass="RecPV_Enum3"	'Cyan
		oShape.objects("ConAlpTarget").styleclass="A_TextLarge"	'Black
	end if
    if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CUST_ADVC_TargetSP_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description    
end sub

sub CUST_K1K2_ADVC_Numeric_AlpPVFormat_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim sFormat,oShape, nNumericFormat
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
      nNumericFormat= trim(oshape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_pvformat"))
   if IsNumeric(nNumericFormat) and instr(nNumericFormat,".")=0 then
	  sFormat="%." & nNumericFormat & "f"
	  if typename(oshape.objects("ConAlpActual"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("ConAlpActual").numericdisplayformat=sFormat
	  if typename(oshape.objects("ConAlpTarget"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("ConAlpTarget").numericdisplayformat=sFormat
   else 
   	  window.external.LogMessage "CUST_K1K2_ADVC_Numeric_AlpPVFormat_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & "Invalid PV Format: " & nNumericFormat 
   end if
   if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CUST_K1K2_ADVC_Numeric_AlpPVFormat_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

'CUST_K1K2_ACT1

Sub CUST_ACT1_Mode_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim iPV,oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.value
   if iPV = "P-AUTO" then
		oShape.objects("RecFrame2").styleclass="RecFrame"	'Gray
		oShape.objects("ScConAlp").styleclass="ScConAlpPV_Enum3"	'Cyan
	elseif iPV = "AUTO" then
		oShape.objects("RecFrame2").styleclass="RecPV_Enum2"	'Yellow
		oShape.objects("ScConAlp").styleclass="A_TextSmall"	'Black
	elseif iPV = "P-MAN" then
		oShape.objects("RecFrame2").styleclass="RecFrame"	'Gray
		oShape.objects("ScConAlp").styleclass="ScConAlpPV_Enum3"	'Cyan
	elseif iPV = "MAN" then
		oShape.objects("RecFrame2").styleclass="RecPV_Enum2"	'Yellow
		oShape.objects("ScConAlp").styleclass="A_TextSmall"	'Black
	elseif iPV = "P-CAS" then
		oShape.objects("RecFrame2").styleclass="RecFrame"	'Gray
		oShape.objects("ScConAlp").styleclass="ScConAlpPV_Enum3"	'Cyan
	elseif iPV = "CAS" then
		oShape.objects("RecFrame2").styleclass="RecPV_Enum3"	'Cyan
		oShape.objects("ScConAlp").styleclass="A_TextSmall"	'Black
	else
		oShape.objects("RecFrame2").styleclass="RecFrame"	'Gray
		oShape.objects("ScConAlp").styleclass="ScConAlpPV_Enum1"	'Red				
	end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CUST_ACT1_Mode_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description    
end sub

Sub CUST_ACT1_SP_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   ModeTag  = oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_act_pnt")
   window.external.stationDataRepository.PutValue "Point_ACT_SP", ModeTag
	if oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_act_trok")>0 and oSource.datavalue("Point_ACT_SP.mode")=oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_act_acm") and oSource.datavalue("Point_ACT_SP.modattr")=oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_act_acma") then
		oShape.objects("RecFrame3").styleclass="RecPV_Enum3"	'Cyan
		oShape.objects("ConAlpSP").styleclass="A_TextLarge"	'Black
	else
		oShape.objects("RecFrame3").styleclass="RecFrame"	'Gray
		oShape.objects("ConAlpSP").styleclass="ConAlpSP"	'Cyan
	end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CUST_ACT1_SP_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description    
end sub

Sub CUST_ACT1_AVG_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape

	else

		oShape.objects("ConAlpPV").styleclass="ScConAlpPV"	'Cyan
	end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CUST_ACT1_AVG_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description    
end sub

Sub CUST_InputBox_SP_OnClick(oSource)
   dim oShape, MaxEntry, MinEntry, Input, Index
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	MaxEntry = csng(oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_act_trlm"))
	MinEntry = csng(-1.0 * MaxEntry)
	Index = Fix(oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_act_trok"))
	window.external.stationDataRepository.PutValue "Point_AC_SP", oSource.DataValue("Tagname.cp_ac_pnt")	'sptr_ent
	window.external.stationDataRepository.PutValue "Para_AC_SP", "sptrim" & "(" & Index & ")"
	Input = InputBox(oShape.objects("ConAlpDesc").value & " SETPOINT CHANGE", "SETPOINT CHANGE ENTRY")
	If IsNumeric(Input) and Len(Input) > 0 then
		Input = csng(Input)
		If Input >= MinEntry And Input <= MaxEntry then
			oSource.datavalue("Point_AC_SP.Para_AC_SP") = Input
		Else
			window.external.TemporaryMessageZoneText "ENTRY OUT OF RANGE"
		End if
	Else
		window.external.TemporaryMessageZoneText "INVALID ENTRY"
	End if
end sub

'-------------------------------------
'CUST_LB
'--------------------------------------
sub CUST_LB_Alp_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape,oExStObj
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   set oExStObj=oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV")
   set oExStObj1=oShape.objects("ConAlpDesc")
   if oSource.DataQuality("tagname.cp_ptexecst") = 1 then
   		oShape.style.visibility = "visible"
	   if oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_ptexecst")=1 then
		  oExStObj.textcolor=oshape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForActive")
		  oExStObj1.textcolor=oshape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForActive")
	   else
		  oExStObj.textcolor=oshape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForInActive")
		  oExStObj1.textcolor=oshape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForInActive")
	   end if
	else
		oShape.style.visibility = "hidden"
	end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "CUST_LB_Alp_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'-------------------------------------
'CUST_CDA_Sootblower_IK
'--------------------------------------

sub CUST_CDA_Sootblower_IK_AlpPV_onUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape,oPVObj
   on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	set oPVObj=oShape.objects("TxtPV")
	set oRecPV =oShape.objects("RecFrame")
	iPV=oSource.value
	index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)

	iQual = oSource.DataQuality("tagname.cp_pv")
if (iQual and iQualityGood)=iQualityGood then
	
	if oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pv") = 6 then
		oPVObj.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & index
		oRecPV.styleclass="RecFrame"
	elseif oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pv") = 5 and oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pv1") = 1 then
		'oPVObj.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & index 'old stuff
		oRecPV.styleclass="RecFrame"
		oPVObj.textColorBlink = "True" 'Added to make inner green text blink
		oPVObj.textColor = vbgreen		'RB 6/20/12
	elseif oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pv") <> 5 and oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pv1") = 1 then
		oPVObj.textcolor= vbblack
		oPVObj.textColorBlink = "False" 'Added to correct black text blink issue RB 6/21/12
		oRecPV.styleclass="RecPV_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV1")	
	elseif oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pv") = 4 then 
		oPVObj.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & index
		oRecPV.styleclass="RecFrame"
		
'	elseif oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pv") = 4 and oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pv1") = 1 then
'		oPVObj.textcolor= vbblack
'		oRecPV.styleclass="RecPV_" & oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV1")	
	else
		oPVObj.styleclass= "ScConAlpPV_Enum4" 'Corrected from Enum04 to Enum4 RB 6/21/12
		oRecPV.styleclass="RecFrame"						
	end if
else
	oPVObj.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForPV" & cstr(oSource.value))
end if
	if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " CUST_CDA_Sootblower_IK_AlpPV_onUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub]]></script></scripts><parameters><parameter name="Revision" type="Num" description="" defaultvalue="R400 Rev.0"/></parameters><shapes><shape><element><html><content><![CDATA[

' Form text representation of HIGHAL parameter
Function AlmTp (iAlmType)
On error resume next
select case iAlmType
	case	0		AlmTp=	""
	case	1		AlmTp=	"INV"
	case	2		AlmTp=	"PPR"
	case	3		AlmTp=	"PRR"
	case	4		AlmTp=	"PRT"
	case	5		AlmTp=	"OFN"
	case	7		AlmTp=	"OPR"
	case	8		AlmTp=	"FLG"
	case	10		AlmTp=	"CH"
	case	11		AlmTp=	"UNR"
	case	12		AlmTp=	"CMD"
	case	18		AlmTp=	"COS"
	case	19		AlmTp=	"S1L"
	case	20		AlmTp=	"S2L"
	case	21		AlmTp=	"S3L"
	case	22		AlmTp=	"S4L"
	case	25		AlmTp=	"OF1"
	case	26		AlmTp=	"OF2"
	case	27		AlmTp=	"OF3"
	case	28		AlmTp=	"ADV"
	case	29		AlmTp=	"NRC"
	case	30		AlmTp=	"PRC"
	case	31		AlmTp=	"PRC"
	case	32		AlmTp=	"DVL"
	case	33		AlmTp=	"DVH"
	case	34		AlmTp=	"DLL"
	case	35		AlmTp=	"DHH"
	case	36		AlmTp=	"LLD"
	case	37		AlmTp=	"HHD"
	case	38		AlmTp=	"L"
	case	39		AlmTp=	"H"
	case	41		AlmTp=	"LL"
	case	42		AlmTp=	"HH"
	case	43		AlmTp=	"LLL"
	case	44		AlmTp=	"HHH"
	case	54		AlmTp=	"SHI"
	case	55		AlmTp=	"SHH"
	case	56		AlmTp=	"BSV"
	case	63		AlmTp=	"CTL"
	case	64		AlmTp=	"BAD"
	case	65		AlmTp=	"UNC"
	case	68		AlmTp=	"FL"
	case	69		AlmTp=	"OV2"
	case	70		AlmTp=	"OV1"
	case	71		AlmTp=	"OV0"
	case	72		AlmTp=	"SAF"
	case	73		AlmTp=	"C1A"
	case	74		AlmTp=	"C2A"
	case	75		AlmTp=	"C3A"
	case	76		AlmTp=	"C4A"
	case	78		AlmTp=	"SAF"
	case	85		AlmTp=	"BOC"
	case	86		AlmTp=	"OPH"
	case	87		AlmTp=	"OPL"
end select
end Function


'--------------------------------------
'DevCtl - Redtag; Hialm Type; 
'--------------------------------------
sub HG_Devctl_Hialm_RedTag_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim iRedTag, oShape, oDevctlAlm
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_highal")<>"" then
   		set oDevctlAlm=oShape.objects("TxtHiAlmType")
   		oDevctlAlm.innertext = AlmTp(oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_highal"))
   end if
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_redtag")<>"" then
   		iRedTag=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_redtag")
   		if iRedTag=1 then
      		oShape.objects("WdgRedTag").styleclass="WdgRedTag_On"
  		else
     		 oShape.objects("WdgRedTag").styleclass="WdgRedTag_Off"
   		end if
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HG_Devctl_Hialm_RedTag_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'--------------------------------------
'Graph objects
'--------------------------------------
sub HG_Graph_AlpError_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape
   On error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)=iQualityGood then
      oShape.objects("TxtError").styleclass="TxtError_NoError"
   else
      oShape.objects("TxtError").styleclass="TxtError_InError"
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HG_Graph_AlpError_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'--------------------------------------------
'3 States Device
'--------------------------------------------
Sub HG_Device3States_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim iPV,oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.value
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   if typename(oShape.objects("OvlBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("OvlBody").styleclass="OvlBody_" & index	   
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgBody").styleclass="PlgBody_" & index
   if typename(oShape.objects("RecBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("RecBody").styleclass="RecBody_" & index
   if iPV=cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_BadPVState")) then
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   else
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HG_Device3States_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description    
end sub

'-------------------------------------
'Digital States
'--------------------------------------
Sub HG_DigStateCText_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape,oPVObj
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   set oPVObj=oShape.objects("TxtPV")
    if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)=iQualityGood then
      oPVObj.innertext=oShape.GetCustomProperty("Text","cp_TextForPV" &  cstr(oSource.value))
      oPVObj.styleclass="ScConAlpPV"
   else
      oPVObj.innertext="--------" 
      oPVObj.textcolor=vbRed
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HG_DigStateCText_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'--------------------------------------
'Numeric Values
'--------------------------------------
sub HG_Numeric_AlpPVFormat_OnUpdate(oSource,data_src)
   dim sFormat,oShape, nNumericFormat
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if ucase(data_src)="SD" then
	  if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_pvformat")<>"" then nNumericFormat= oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_pvformat")
   else
      nNumericFormat= trim(oshape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_pvformat"))
   end if
   if IsNumeric(nNumericFormat) and instr(nNumericFormat,".")=0 then
	  sFormat="%." & nNumericFormat & "f"
	  if typename(oshape.objects("ScConAlpPV"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").numericdisplayformat=sFormat
	  if typename(oshape.objects("ConAlpSP"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("ConAlpSP").numericdisplayformat=sFormat
   else 
   	  window.external.LogMessage "HG_Numeric_AlpPVFormat_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & "Invalid PV Format: " & nNumericFormat 
   end if
   if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HG_Numeric_AlpPVFormat_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

'--------------------------------------
'RegCntl - Mode; Redtag; Hialm Type; EUDesc; 
'--------------------------------------
sub HG_RegCntl_Mode_Hialm_RedTag_EUDesc_OnUpdate(oSource,iLen)
   dim sMode, oMode, sNMode, iAlmEnbState, iRedTag, oShape, oPIDAlm, iInitMan,iArwop, iNModeAttr, iModeAttr
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if typename(oShape.objects("TxtMode"))<>"Nothing" then
	   set oMode=oShape.objects("TxtMode")
	   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_mode")<>"" then
	      sMode=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_mode")
	      oMode.innertext=left(smode,ilen)
	   end if
	   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_nmode")<>"" then
	      sNMode=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_nmode")
		  if sMode=sNMode then
	      	oMode.styleclass="TxtMode_in_NMode"
	      else 
	      	oMode.styleclass="TxtMode_not_in_NMode"
	      end if 
	   end if
	   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_nmodeAttr")<>"" then
		  iNModeAttr=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_nmodeAttr")
		  iModeAttr=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_modeAttr")	
		  if iNModeAttr<>0 then
		   	  if iModeAttr=iNModeAttr then
		      	oMode.linecolor="transparent"
		      else 
		      	oMode.linecolor=oMode.getstyleproperty("mAttrProgF") 
				oMode.linestyle=1
			    oMode.linewidth=oMode.getstyleproperty("lWidth")
		      end if 
			  if iModeAttr=2 then oMode.textcolor = oMode.getstyleproperty("mAttrProgL")
           end if
	   end if		
   end if
   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_highal")<>"" then 
		oShape.objects("TxtHiAlmType").innertext = AlmTp(oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_highal"))
   end if
   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_redtag")<>"" then 
  	 iRedTag=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_redtag")
  	 if iRedTag=1 then
  	    oShape.objects("WdgRedTag").styleclass="WdgRedTag_On"
 	 else
	    oShape.objects("WdgRedTag").styleclass="WdgRedTag_Off"
   	 end if
   end if
   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_initman")<>"" then
      set oInit = oShape.objects("TxtInit") 
	  set oWindup = oShape.objects("LinWR")
	  iInitMan = oSource.datavalue ("tagname.cp_initman")
	  oWindup.styleclass = "LinWR_No_WindupReset"
	  	if iInitMan = 1 then
			oInit.styleclass = "Init_Active"
			oWindup.styleclass = "LinWR_No_WindupReset"
		else
			oInit.styleclass = "Init_Inactive"
			if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_arwop")<>"" then
				iArwop = oSource.datavalue ("tagname.cp_arwop")
				select case iArwop
				     case 1 oWindup.styleclass="LinWR_Windup_Hi"
				     case 2 oWindup.styleclass="LinWR_Windup_LO"
				     case 3 oWindup.styleclass="LinWR_Windup_HILO"
				end select
            end if  
		end if
   end if
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_TagnameEU")="" then oShape.objects("TxtEUDesc").innertext = oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_eudesc")
   if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " HG_RegCntl_Mode_Hialm_RedTag_EUDesc_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

'-------------------------------------
'Controller with Deviation
'--------------------------------------
sub HG_RegCntl_AlpDev_OnUpdate(oSource)
   on error resume next
   dim rDev, rRange,oShape
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_dev")<>"" then rDev=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_dev") 
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_pveuhi")<>"" and oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_pveulo")<>"" then
   		rRange=0.05*(oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_pveuhi")-oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_pveulo"))
   end if
   rRange=csng(oShape.getcustomproperty("Real","RangeDev"))
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_advdevalm.tp")<>"" then 
   	  if instr(oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_advdevalm.tp"),"N")=0 then	rRange=csng(oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_advdevalm.tp"))
   end if 
   oShape.objects("IndDev").value=(rDev*100/rRange)
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HG_RegCntl_AlpDev_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

'-------------------------------------------------------
'OnOffValves
'-------------------------------------------------------
Sub HG_OnOffValves_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim iPV,oShape,iOP,index,indexOP
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.value
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgIn")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index	   
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgOut")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_op")<>"" then
   		iOP = oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_op")
		indexOP = oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iOP)
		if typename(oShape.objects("WdgC")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_" & indexOP
   end if
   if iPV=cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_BadPVState")) then
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   else
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HG_OnOffValves_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'----------------------------------------------------------------
'RegCtl Valves
'----------------------------------------------------------------
Sub HG_RegCntlValve_AlpVal_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim rVal,rClosed,oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   rVal=oSource.value
   rClosed=csng(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","ClosedValue"))
   sRange = lcase(page.GetStyleClassProperty("UseExtended_RegValve_range","key"))
   if sRange <>"yes" then
	   if rVal>100 then 
	      rVal=100
	   elseif rVal<0 then 
	      rVal=0
	   end if
   end if
   if oShape.objects("TxtOP") is nothing then
      oShape.objects("IndVal").value=rVal
   else
	  oShape.objects("TxtOP").innertext=round(rVal) & "%"	
   end if
   if rVal > rClosed then
	  index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForOpen" )
   else
	  index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForClose" )
   end if
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgIn")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index	   
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgOut")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HG_RegCntlValve_AlpVal_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

end sub

   end if
