end sub

'---------------------------------------
'3Way valves
'---------------------------------------
Sub HG_Valve3Way_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim iPV,oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.value
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgIn")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index	   
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgOut")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgOut1")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgOut1").styleclass="PlgOut1_" & index
   if iPV=cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_BadPVState")) then
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   else
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad" 
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HG_Valve3Way_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'--------------------------------------
'Bar Shapes 
'--------------------------------------

sub HG_Bar_OnUpdate(oSource)
    dim oShape,cpRangeHI,cpRangeLO,rangeHI,rangeLO,PriorityL,PriorityH
	dim Ind_H,Ind_L,sColor,PV,SP,TripH,TripL
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_mode")<>"" then 
		sMode=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_mode")
		oShape.objects("TxtMode").innertext=left(sMode,1)
    end if
	if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_nmode")<>"" then
      sNMode=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_nmode")
	  if sMode=sNMode then
      	oShape.objects("TxtMode").styleclass="TxtMode_in_NMode"
      else 
      	oShape.objects("TxtMode").styleclass="TxtMode_not_in_NMode"
      end if 
    end if
    rangeHI=oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_pveuhi")
    rangeLO=oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_pveulo")
	cpRangeHI=oShape.getcustomproperty("Real","cp_rangeHI")
	if cpRangeHI="" then cpRangeHI=rangeHI
    cpRangeLO=oShape.getcustomproperty("Real","cp_rangeLO")
	if cpRangeLO="" then cpRangeLO=rangeLO
    PV=oSource.value
	if csng(PV)<csng(cpRangeLO) then PV=cpRangeLO
	if csng(PV)>csng(cpRangeHI) then PV=cpRangeHI
    oShape.objects("IndPV").value=(PV-cpRangeLO)/(cpRangeHI-cpRangeLO)*100
    if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_sp")<>"" and typename(oShape.objects("IndSP"))<>"Nothing" then
		SP=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_sp")
		if instr(SP,"N")=0 then 
			oShape.objects("IndSP").style.visibility="inherit"
			if csng(SP)<csng(cpRangeLO) then SP=cpRangeLO
			if csng(SP)>csng(cpRangeHI) then SP=cpRangeHI
    		oShape.objects("IndSP").value=(SP-cpRangeLO)/(cpRangeHI-cpRangeLO)*100
        else
			oShape.objects("IndSP").style.visibility="hidden"
		end if 
    end if
	if typename(oshape.objects("IndH"))<>"Nothing" and typename(oshape.objects("IndL"))<>"Nothing" then
		set Ind_H=oShape.objects("IndH")
		set Ind_L=oShape.objects("IndL")
		PriorityH=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvhialm.pr")
		if PriorityH="LOW" or PriorityH="HIGH" or PriorityH="EMERGNCY" then
		    if PriorityH="EMERGNCY" then PriorityH="URGENT"
		    sColor="AlarmStateIconFactory." & PriorityH & "Color"
		    Ind_H.levelfillcolor=eval(sColor)
			Ind_H.Style.visibility="inherit"
			TripH=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvhialm.tp")
			if csng(TripH)>csng(cpRangeHI) then TripH=cpRangeHI
			if csng(TripH)<csng(cpRangeLO) then TripH=cpRangeLO  
	        oShape.objects("IndH").value=100-(TripH-cpRangeLO)/(cpRangeHI-cpRangeLO)*100
		end if
		PriorityL=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvloalm.pr")
		if PriorityL="LOW" or PriorityL="HIGH" or PriorityL="EMERGNCY" then
		    if PriorityL="EMERGNCY" then PriorityL="URGENT"
		    sColor="AlarmStateIconFactory." & PriorityL & "Color"
		    Ind_L.levelfillcolor=eval(sColor)
			Ind_L.Style.visibility="inherit"
			TripL=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvloalm.tp")
			if csng(TripL)>csng(cpRangeHI) then TripL=cpRangeHI
			if csng(TripL)<csng(cpRangeLO) then TripL=cpRangeLO  
		    oShape.objects("IndL").value=(TripL-cpRangeLO)/(cpRangeHI-cpRangeLO)*100
		end if
	end if
	if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_pvsts")<>"" then
   		iPvSts = oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_pvsts")
   		if iPvSts > 1 then
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   		else
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   		end if
    end if
    if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HG_Bar_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub HG_DevBar_OnUpdate(oSource)
    dim oShape,cpRange,DevHI,DevLO,PriorityDevHI,PriorityDevLO
	dim Ind_DevHI,Ind_DevLO,sColor,DEV,TripDevHI,TripDevLO
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	sMode=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_mode")
	oShape.objects("TxtMode").innertext=left(sMode,1)
	if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_nmode")<>"" then
      sNMode=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_nmode")
	  if sMode=sNMode then
      	oShape.objects("TxtMode").styleclass="TxtMode_in_NMode"
      else 
      	oShape.objects("TxtMode").styleclass="TxtMode_not_in_NMode"
      end if 
    end if 
	cpRange=oShape.getcustomproperty("Real","cp_range")
	if cpRange="" then cpRange=(oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pveuhi")-oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pveulo"))*0.1 
    DEV=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_dev")
	oShape.objects("IndDEV").value=DEV*100/cpRange
	if typename(oshape.objects("IndDevH"))<>"Nothing"  and typename(oshape.objects("IndDevL"))<>"Nothing" then
		set Ind_DevHI=oShape.objects("IndDevH")
		set Ind_DevLO=oShape.objects("IndDevL")
		PriorityDevHI=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_devhialm.pr") 
		if PriorityDevHI="LOW" or PriorityDevHI="HIGH" or PriorityDevHI="EMERGNCY" then
		    if PriorityDevHI="EMERGNCY" then PriorityDevHI="URGENT"
	    	sColor="AlarmStateIconFactory." & PriorityDevHI & "Color"
	    	Ind_DevHI.levelfillcolor=eval(sColor)
			Ind_DevHI.Style.visibility="inherit"
			TripDevHI=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_devhialm.tp")
		end if    
    	PriorityDevLO=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_devloalm.pr")
		if PriorityDevLO="LOW" or PriorityDevLO="HIGH" or PriorityDevLO="EMERGNCY" then
		    if PriorityDevLO="EMERGNCY" then PriorityDevLO="URGENT"
	    	sColor="AlarmStateIconFactory." & PriorityDevLO & "Color"
	    	Ind_DevLO.levelfillcolor=eval(sColor)
			Ind_DevLO.Style.visibility="inherit"
			TripDevLO=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_devloalm.tp")
		end if
		Ind_DevHI.value=(cpRange-TripDevHI)*100/cpRange
    	Ind_DevLO.value=(cpRange-abs(TripDevLO))*100/cpRange
	end if
	if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_pvsts")<>"" then
   		iPvSts = oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_pvsts")
   		if iPvSts > 1 then
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   		else
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   		end if
    end if		
    if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HG_DevBar_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub HG_Switch_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource) 
   dim iPV,oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.value
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   if typename(oShape.objects("LinSw0")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("LinSw0").styleclass="LinSw0_" & index	   
   if typename(oShape.objects("LinSw1")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("LinSw1").styleclass="LinSw1_" & index	   
   if iPV=cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_BadPVState")) then
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   else
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " HG_Switch_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description    
end sub

sub HG_Switch3P_AlpPAR_OnUpdate(oSource) 
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	Set PolTL = oSource.ParentNode.ParentNode.Objects("PolTL")
	Set PolTR = oSource.ParentNode.ParentNode.Objects("PolTR")
	Set PolBL = oSource.ParentNode.ParentNode.Objects("PolBL")
	Set PolBR = oSource.ParentNode.ParentNode.Objects("PolBR")
	Set LinH = oSource.ParentNode.ParentNode.Objects("LinH")
	Set LinV = oSource.ParentNode.ParentNode.Objects("LinV")
	PolTL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_Off"
	PolTR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_Off"
	PolBL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_Off"
	PolBR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_Off"
	LinH.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_Off"
	LinV.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_Off"
	for i=1 to 3 
   		If ucase(oSource.value) = ucase(oShape.GetCustomProperty("Text", "cp_Value" & i)) or i=3  Then        	
			Select case UCase(oShape.GetCustomProperty("Text", "cp_Position" & i))
				Case "TL"
					PolTL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "TR"
					PolTR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "BL"
					PolBL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "BR"
					PolBR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "H"
					LinH.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "V"
					LinV.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "TLBR"
					PolTL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					PolBR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "TRBL"
					PolTR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					PolBL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case ""
				Case Else
					PolTL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					PolTR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					PolBL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					PolBR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					LinH.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					LinV.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"			
			end select
			exit for
		end if
	next		 
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " HG_Switch3P_AlpPAR_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description    
end sub

sub HG_Procop_Alp_OnUpdate(oSource)
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	state=oSource.value
	instpend=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_instpend")
	prcmod = oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_procmod")
	
	set oSeqState=oSHape.objects("seqState")
	set oSeqSts=oSHape.objects("seqExecSts")
	set oFrame=oSHape.objects("PlgFrame")
	oSeqSts.value =1
	oSeqState.value =1
	if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then 
		oSeqState.value =2
	else
		oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame"
		select case state
			case 0,1,2 oSeqState.value=1
			case 3 oSeqState.value=12
            case 4 oSeqState.value=10:oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame_Running"
			case 5 oSeqSts.value =6:oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame_Running"
			case 6 oSeqSts.value =5:oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame_Running"
			case 7 oSeqState.value=3:oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame_Running"
	    end select 
	end if
    if instpend=1 then oSeqSts.value=2
	if prcmod=4 then 
		oSeqState.value=9
	elseif prcmod=5 or prcmod=6 then 
		oSeqState.value=11
	end if

	if oShape.style.pixelwidth <>196 then
		iLeft = oShape.objects("ScTxtSelectBox").style.pixelleft
		iWidth = oShape.objects("ScTxtSelectBox").style.pixelwidth
		oShape.objects("RecFrame").style.pixelleft = iLeft+2
		oShape.objects("RecFrame").style.pixelwidth = iWidth-4
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").style.pixelleft = iLeft+6
		iY1 = oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(1).y
		iY2 = oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(2).y
		iY3 = oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(3).y
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(3).setnodepoint iWidth-18,iY3
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(2).setnodepoint iWidth-6,iY2
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(1).setnodepoint iWidth-18,iY1
		oShape.objects("seqState").style.pixelleft = iLeft+7
		oShape.objects("seqState").style.pixelwidth = 16
		oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").style.pixelleft = iLeft+iWidth-33
		oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").style.pixelwidth = 22
		oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelleft = iLeft+iWidth-56
		oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelwidth = 16
		oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelleft = iLeft+24
		oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelwidth = oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelleft-oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelleft
	end if
	if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HPM_Procop_Alp_OnUpdate->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

   if typename(oShape.objects("LvrBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("LvrBody").styleclass="LvrBody_" & index 	
   if iPV=cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_BadPVState")) then
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   else
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   end if
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_op")<>"" then
   		iOP = oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_op")

		if typename(oShape.objects("LvrMFold")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("LvrMfold").styleclass = "LvrMfold_" & indexOP
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " HG_Louvers_AlpGPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

' Form text representation of HIGHAL parameter
Function AlmTp (iAlmType)
On error resume next
select case iAlmType
	case	0		AlmTp=	""
	case	1		AlmTp=	"INV"
	case	2		AlmTp=	"PPR"
	case	3		AlmTp=	"PRR"
	case	4		AlmTp=	"PRT"
	case	5		AlmTp=	"OFN"
	case	7		AlmTp=	"OPR"
	case	8		AlmTp=	"FLG"
	case	10		AlmTp=	"CH"
	case	11		AlmTp=	"UNR"
	case	12		AlmTp=	"CMD"
	case	18		AlmTp=	"COS"
	case	19		AlmTp=	"S1L"
	case	20		AlmTp=	"S2L"
	case	21		AlmTp=	"S3L"
	case	22		AlmTp=	"S4L"
	case	25		AlmTp=	"OF1"
	case	26		AlmTp=	"OF2"
	case	27		AlmTp=	"OF3"
	case	28		AlmTp=	"ADV"
	case	29		AlmTp=	"NRC"
	case	30		AlmTp=	"PRC"
	case	31		AlmTp=	"ROC"
	case	32		AlmTp=	"DVL"
	case	33		AlmTp=	"DVH"
	case	34		AlmTp=	"DLL"
	case	35		AlmTp=	"DHH"
	case	36		AlmTp=	"LLD"
	case	37		AlmTp=	"HHD"
	case	38		AlmTp=	"L"
	case	39		AlmTp=	"H"
	case	41		AlmTp=	"LL"
	case	42		AlmTp=	"HH"
	case	43		AlmTp=	"LLL"
	case	44		AlmTp=	"HHH"
	case	54		AlmTp=	"SHI"
	case	55		AlmTp=	"SHH"
	case	56		AlmTp=	"BSV"
	case	63		AlmTp=	"CTL"
	case	64		AlmTp=	"BAD"
	case	65		AlmTp=	"UNC"
	case	68		AlmTp=	"FL"
	case	69		AlmTp=	"OV2"
	case	70		AlmTp=	"OV1"
	case	71		AlmTp=	"OV0"
	case	72		AlmTp=	"SAF"
	case	73		AlmTp=	"C1A"
	case	74		AlmTp=	"C2A"
	case	75		AlmTp=	"C3A"
	case	76		AlmTp=	"C4A"
	case	78		AlmTp=	"SAF"
	case	85		AlmTp=	"BOC"
	case	86		AlmTp=	"OPH"
	case	87		AlmTp=	"OPL"
end select
end Function

'--------------------------------------
'RegCntl - Mode; Redtag; Hialm Type; EUDesc; 
'--------------------------------------
sub HPM_RegCntl_Mode_Hialm_RedTag_EUDesc_OnUpdate(oSource,iLen)
   dim sMode, oMode, sNMode, iAlmEnbState, iRedTag, oShape,oPIDAlm, oInitWindup, iInitMan,iArwop, iNModeAttr, iModeAttr
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode 
   if typename(oShape.objects("TxtMode"))<>"Nothing" then
	   set oMode=oShape.objects("TxtMode")
	   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_mode")<>"" then
	      sMode=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_mode")
	      oMode.innertext=left(smode,ilen)
	   end if
	   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_nmode")<>"" then
	      sNMode=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_nmode")
		  if sMode=sNMode then
	      	oMode.styleclass="TxtMode_in_NMode"
	      else 
	      	oMode.styleclass="TxtMode_not_in_NMode"
	      end if 
	   end if
	   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_nmodeAttr")<>"" then
		  iNModeAttr=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_nmodeAttr")
		  iModeAttr=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_modeAttr")	
		  if iNModeAttr<>0 then
		   	  if iModeAttr=iNModeAttr then
		      	oMode.linecolor="transparent"
		      else 
		      	oMode.linecolor=oMode.getstyleproperty("mAttrProgF") 
				oMode.linestyle=1
			    oMode.linewidth=oMode.getstyleproperty("lWidth")
		      end if 
			  if iModeAttr=2 then oMode.textcolor = oMode.getstyleproperty("mAttrProgL")
           end if
	   end if		
   end if
   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_highal")<>"" then 
		oShape.objects("TxtHiAlmType").innertext = AlmTp(oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_highal"))
   end if
   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_redtag")<>"" then 
  	 iRedTag=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_redtag")
  	 if iRedTag=1 then
  	    oShape.objects("WdgRedTag").styleclass="WdgRedTag_On"
 	 else
	    oShape.objects("WdgRedTag").styleclass="WdgRedTag_Off"
   	 end if
   end if
   if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_initman")<>"" then
      set oInit = oShape.objects("TxtInit") 
	  set oWindup = oShape.objects("LinWR")
	  iInitMan = oSource.datavalue ("tagname.cp_initman")
	  oWindup.styleclass = "LinWR_No_WindupReset"
	  	if iInitMan = 1 then
			oInit.styleclass = "Init_Active"
			oWindup.styleclass = "LinWR_No_WindupReset"
		else
			oInit.styleclass = "Init_Inactive"
			if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_sptvstate")<>"" then
				iTVstate=oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_sptvstate")
				iTV=oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_sptv")
				if iTVState=2 then
					if typename(oShape.objects("ConAlpSP"))<>"Nothing" then
						rSP=oShape.objects("ConAlpSP").value	
                    else
                        if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_dev")<>"" then
							rSP=oSource.value-oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_dev")
                        else
							rSP=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_sp")
						end if
					end if 	
				    if iTV>rSP then oWindup.styleclass="LinWR_Ramp_HI"
					if iTV<rSP then oWindup.styleclass="LinWR_Ramp_LO"
                else
					oWindup.styleclass="LinWR_No_WindupReset"
				end if
            end if
			if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_arwop")<>"" then
				iArwop = oSource.datavalue ("tagname.cp_arwop")
				select case iArwop
				     case 1 oWindup.styleclass="LinWR_Windup_Hi"
				     case 2 oWindup.styleclass="LinWR_Windup_LO"
				     case 3 oWindup.styleclass="LinWR_Windup_HILO"
				end select
            end if  
		end if
   end if
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_TagnameEU")="" then oShape.objects("TxtEUDesc").innertext = oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_eudesc")
   if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " HPM_RegCntl_Mode_Hialm_RedTag_EUDesc_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

'--------------------------------------
'Numeric Values
'--------------------------------------
sub HPM_Numeric_AlpPVFormat_OnUpdate(oSource,data_src)
   dim sFormat,oShape, nNumericFormat
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if ucase(data_src)="SD" then
	  if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_pvformat")<>"" then nNumericFormat= oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_pvformat")
   else
      nNumericFormat= trim(oshape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_pvformat"))
   end if
   if IsNumeric(nNumericFormat) and instr(nNumericFormat,".")=0 then
	  sFormat="%." & nNumericFormat & "f"
	  if typename(oshape.objects("ScConAlpPV"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("ScConAlpPV").numericdisplayformat=sFormat
	  if typename(oshape.objects("ConAlpSP"))<>"Nothing" then oShape.objects("ConAlpSP").numericdisplayformat=sFormat
   else 
   	  window.external.LogMessage "HPM_Numeric_AlpPVFormat_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & "Invalid PV Format: " & nNumericFormat 
   end if
   if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HPM_Numeric_AlpPVFormat_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

'-------------------------------------
'Controller with Deviation
'--------------------------------------
sub HPM_RegCntl_AlpDev_OnUpdate(oSource)
   on error resume next
   dim rDev, rRange,oShape
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_dev")<>"" then rDev=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_dev") 
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_pveuhi")<>"" and oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_pveulo")<>"" then
   		rRange=0.05*(oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_pveuhi")-oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_pveulo"))
   end if
   rRange=csng(oShape.getcustomproperty("Real","RangeDev"))
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_advdevalm.tp")<>"" then 
   	  if instr(oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_advdevalm.tp"),"N")=0 then	rRange=csng(oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_advdevalm.tp"))
   end if 
   oShape.objects("IndDev").value=(rDev*100/rRange)
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HPM_RegCntl_AlpDev_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

'-------------------------------------
'Digital States
'--------------------------------------
sub HPM_ExecState_Alp_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape,oExStObj
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   set oExStObj=oShape.objects("LinExecState")
   if oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_ptexecst")=1 then
	  oExStObj.styleclass="LinExecState_OnScan_Active"
   else
	  oExStObj.styleclass="LinExecState_OffScan_InActive"
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HPM_ExecState_Alp_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

Sub HPM_DigStateCText_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape,oPVObj
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   set oPVObj=oShape.objects("TxtPV")
    if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)=iQualityGood then
      oPVObj.innertext=oShape.GetCustomProperty("Text","cp_TextForPV" &  cstr(oSource.value))
	  oPVObj.styleclass="ScConAlpPV_" & oShape.getcustomproperty("Style","cp_StyleForPV" & cstr(oSource.value))
   else
      oPVObj.innertext="--------" 
      oPVObj.textcolor=vbRed
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HPM_DigStateCText_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

Sub HPM_DigStateCColor_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.value
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   oShape.objects("RecPV").styleclass="RecPV_" & index	
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HPM_DigStateCColor_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description       
end sub

   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.breakpointvalueenum

   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HPM_DigState_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description       
end sub
'--------------------------------------
'DevCtl - Redtag; Hialm Type; 
'--------------------------------------
sub HPM_Devctl_Hialm_RedTag_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim iRedTag, oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_highal")<>"" then
   		oShape.objects("TxtHiAlmType").innertext = AlmTp(oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_highal"))
   end if
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_redtag")<>"" then
   		iRedTag=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_redtag")
   		if iRedTag=1 then
      		oShape.objects("WdgRedTag").styleclass="WdgRedTag_On"
  		else
     		 oShape.objects("WdgRedTag").styleclass="WdgRedTag_Off"
   		end if
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HPM_Devctl_Hialm_RedTag_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'--------------------------------------
'Graph objects
'--------------------------------------
sub HPM_Graph_AlpError_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape
   On error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)=iQualityGood then
      oShape.objects("TxtError").styleclass="TxtError_NoError"
   else
      oShape.objects("TxtError").styleclass="TxtError_InError"
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HPM_Graph_AlpError_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'--------------------------------------------
'3 States Device
'--------------------------------------------
Sub HPM_Device3States_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim iPV,oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.value
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   if typename(oShape.objects("OvlBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("OvlBody").styleclass="OvlBody_" & index	   
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgBody").styleclass="PlgBody_" & index
   if typename(oShape.objects("RecBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("RecBody").styleclass="RecBody_" & index
   if iPV=cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_BadPVState")) then
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   else
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HPM_Device3States_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description    
end sub

'----------------------------------------------------------------
'RegCtl Valves
'----------------------------------------------------------------
Sub HPM_RegCntlValve_AlpVal_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim rVal,rClosed,oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   rVal=oSource.value
   rClosed=csng(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","ClosedValue"))
   sRange = lcase(page.GetStyleClassProperty("UseExtended_RegValve_range","key"))
   if sRange <>"yes" then
	   if rVal>100 then 
	      rVal=100
	   elseif rVal<0 then 
	      rVal=0
	   end if
   end if
   if oShape.objects("TxtOP") is nothing then
      oShape.objects("IndVal").value=rVal
   else
	  oShape.objects("TxtOP").innertext=round(rVal) & "%"	
   end if
   if rVal > rClosed then
	  index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForOpen" )
   else
	  index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForClose" )
   end if
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgIn")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index	   
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgOut")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HPM_RegCntlValve_AlpVal_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub HPM_RegCtlValves_RedTag_BadCtlFl_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim iRedTag, iBadCtlFl, oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_redtag")<>"" then
   		iRedTag=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_redtag")
   		if iRedTag=1 then
      		oShape.objects("WdgRedTag").styleclass="WdgRedTag_On"
   		else
      		oShape.objects("WdgRedTag").styleclass="WdgRedTag_Off"
   		end if
   end if
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_badctlfl")<>"" then
   		iBadCtlFl=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_badctlfl")
   		if iBadCtlFl>0 then
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   		else
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   		end if
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HPM_RegCtlValves_RedTag_BadCtlFl_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description     
end sub

sub HPM_RegCtlValves_PvSts_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim iPvSts, oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_pvsts")<>"" then
   		iPvSts = oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_pvsts")
   		if iPvSts > 1 then
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   		else
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   		end if
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HPM_RegCtlValves_PvSts_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'-------------------------------------------------------
'OnOffValves
'-------------------------------------------------------
Sub HPM_OnOffValves_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim iPV,oShape,iOP,index,indexOP
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.value
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgIn")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index	   
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgOut")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_op")<>"" then
   		iOP = oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_op")
		indexOP = oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iOP)
		if typename(oShape.objects("WdgC")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_" & indexOP
   end if
   if iPV=cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_BadPVState")) then
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   else
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HPM_OnOffValves_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub HPM_MOV_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource) 
   dim iPV,oShape,iOP,index,indexOP
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.value 
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgIn")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index	   
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgOut")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
   if iPV=cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_BadPVState")) then
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   else
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   end if
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_op")<>"" then
   		iOP = oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_op")
		indexOP = oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iOP)
		if typename(oShape.objects("WdgC")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("WdgC").styleclass = "WdgC_" & indexOP
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " HPM_MOV_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub HPM_MOVInd_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource) 
   dim oShape,rVal
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   rVal=oSource.value
   if isnumeric(rVal) then
   		if rVal>100 then 
      		rVal=100
   		elseif rVal<0 then 
      		rVal=0
   		end if
	else
		rVal=100
   end if
   if oShape.objects("TxtOP") is nothing then
	  oShape.objects("IndVal").value=rVal
   else
	  oShape.objects("TxtOP").innertext=round(rVal) & "%"	
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " HPM_MOVInd_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

Sub HPM_Valve3Way_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim iPV,oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.value
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgIn")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgIn").styleclass="PlgIn_" & index	   
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgOut")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgOut").styleclass="PlgOut_" & index
   if typename(oShape.objects("PlgOut1")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("PlgOut1").styleclass="PlgOut1_" & index
   if iPV=cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_BadPVState")) then
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   else
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad" 
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HPM_Valve3Way_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub


'--------------------------------------
'Bar Shapes 
'--------------------------------------

sub HPM_Bar_OnUpdate(oSource)
    dim oShape,cpRangeHI,cpRangeLO,rangeHI,rangeLO, PriorityLL,PriorityL,PriorityH,PriorityHH
	dim Ind_HH,Ind_H,Ind_L,Ind_LL,sColor,PV,SP,TripHH,TripH,TripL,TripLL
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_mode")<>"" then
		sMode=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_mode")
		oShape.objects("TxtMode").innertext=left(sMode,1)
	end if
	if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_nmode")<>"" then
      sNMode=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_nmode")
	  if sMode=sNMode then
      	oShape.objects("TxtMode").styleclass="TxtMode_in_NMode"
      else 
      	oShape.objects("TxtMode").styleclass="TxtMode_not_in_NMode"
      end if 
    end if
    rangeHI=oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_pveuhi")
    rangeLO=oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_pveulo")
	cpRangeHI=oShape.getcustomproperty("Real","cp_rangeHI")
	if cpRangeHI="" then cpRangeHI=rangeHI
    cpRangeLO=oShape.getcustomproperty("Real","cp_rangeLO")
	if cpRangeLO="" then cpRangeLO=rangeLO
    PV=oSource.value
	if csng(PV)<csng(cpRangeLO) then PV=cpRangeLO
	if csng(PV)>csng(cpRangeHI) then PV=cpRangeHI
    oShape.objects("IndPV").value=(PV-cpRangeLO)/(cpRangeHI-cpRangeLO)*100
    if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_sp")<>"" and typename(oShape.objects("IndSP"))<>"Nothing" then
		SP=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_sp")
		if instr(SP,"N")=0 then
			oShape.objects("IndSP").style.visibility="inherit"
			if csng(SP)<csng(cpRangeLO) then SP=cpRangeLO
			if csng(SP)>csng(cpRangeHI) then SP=cpRangeHI
    		oShape.objects("IndSP").value=(SP-cpRangeLO)/(cpRangeHI-cpRangeLO)*100
		else
        	oShape.objects("IndSP").style.visibility="hidden"
        end if
	end if
	if typename(oshape.objects("IndHH"))<>"Nothing" and typename(oshape.objects("IndH"))<>"Nothing" and typename(oshape.objects("IndL"))<>"Nothing" and typename(oshape.objects("IndLL"))<>"Nothing" then
	    set Ind_HH=oShape.objects("IndHH")
		set Ind_H=oShape.objects("IndH")
		set Ind_L=oShape.objects("IndL")
		set Ind_LL=oShape.objects("IndLL")
		PriorityHH=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvhhalm.pr")
		if PriorityHH="LOW" or PriorityHH="HIGH" or PriorityHH="EMERGNCY" then
	    	if PriorityHH="EMERGNCY" then PriorityHH="URGENT"
		    sColor="AlarmStateIconFactory." & PriorityHH & "Color"
		    Ind_HH.levelfillcolor=eval(sColor)
			Ind_HH.Style.visibility="inherit"
			TripHH=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvhhalm.tp")
			if csng(TripHH)>csng(cpRangeHI) then TripHH=cpRangeHI
			if csng(TripHH)<csng(cpRangeLO) then TripHH=cpRangeLO  
	        oShape.objects("IndHH").value=100-(TripHH-cpRangeLO)/(cpRangeHI-cpRangeLO)*100
		end if    
		PriorityH=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvhialm.pr")
		if PriorityH="LOW" or PriorityH="HIGH" or PriorityH="EMERGNCY" then
		    if PriorityH="EMERGNCY" then PriorityH="URGENT"
		    sColor="AlarmStateIconFactory." & PriorityH & "Color"
		    Ind_H.levelfillcolor=eval(sColor)
			Ind_H.Style.visibility="inherit"
			TripH=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvhialm.tp")
			if csng(TripH)>csng(cpRangeHI) then TripH=cpRangeHI
			if csng(TripH)<csng(cpRangeLO) then TripH=cpRangeLO  
	        oShape.objects("IndH").value=100-(TripH-cpRangeLO)/(cpRangeHI-cpRangeLO)*100
		end if
		PriorityL=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvloalm.pr")
		if PriorityL="LOW" or PriorityL="HIGH" or PriorityL="EMERGNCY" then
		    if PriorityL="EMERGNCY" then PriorityL="URGENT"
		    sColor="AlarmStateIconFactory." & PriorityL & "Color"
		    Ind_L.levelfillcolor=eval(sColor)
			Ind_L.Style.visibility="inherit"
			TripL=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvloalm.tp")
			if csng(TripL)>csng(cpRangeHI) then TripL=cpRangeHI
			if csng(TripL)<csng(cpRangeLO) then TripL=cpRangeLO  
		    oShape.objects("IndL").value=(TripL-cpRangeLO)/(cpRangeHI-cpRangeLO)*100
		end if
		PriorityLL=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvllalm.pr")
		if PriorityLL="LOW" or PriorityLL="HIGH" or PriorityLL="EMERGNCY" then
		    if PriorityLL="EMERGNCY" then PriorityLL="URGENT"
		    sColor="AlarmStateIconFactory." & PriorityLL & "Color"
		    Ind_LL.levelfillcolor=eval(sColor)
			Ind_LL.Style.visibility="inherit"
			TripLL=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pvllalm.tp")
			if csng(TripLL)>csng(cpRangeHI) then TripLL=cpRangeHI
			if csng(TripLL)<csng(cpRangeLO) then TripLL=cpRangeLO  
		    oShape.objects("IndLL").value=(TripLL-cpRangeLO)/(cpRangeHI-cpRangeLO)*100
		end if
	end if
	if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_badctlfl")<>"" then
   		iBadCtlFl=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_badctlfl")
   		if iBadCtlFl>0 then
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   		else
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   		end if
    elseif oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_pvsts")<>"" then 
        iPvSts = oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_pvsts")
   		if iPvSts > 1 then
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   		else
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   		end if 
    end if
    if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HPM_Bar_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

sub HPM_DevBar_OnUpdate(oSource)
    dim oShape,cpRange,DevHI,DevLO,PriorityDevHI,PriorityDevLO
	dim Ind_DevHI,Ind_DevLO,sColor,DEV,TripDevHI,TripDevLO
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	sMode=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_mode")
	oShape.objects("TxtMode").innertext=left(sMode,1)
	if oshape.getcustomproperty("parameter","cp_nmode")<>"" then
      sNMode=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_nmode")
	  if sMode=sNMode then
      	oShape.objects("TxtMode").styleclass="TxtMode_in_NMode"
      else 
      	oShape.objects("TxtMode").styleclass="TxtMode_not_in_NMode"
      end if 
    end if 
	cpRange=oShape.getcustomproperty("Real","cp_range")
	if cpRange="" then cpRange=(oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pveuhi")-oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_pveulo"))*0.1 
    DEV=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_dev")
	oShape.objects("IndDEV").value=DEV*100/cpRange
	if typename(oshape.objects("IndDevH"))<>"Nothing"  and typename(oshape.objects("IndDevL"))<>"Nothing" then
		set Ind_DevHI=oShape.objects("IndDevH")
		set Ind_DevLO=oShape.objects("IndDevL")
		PriorityDevHI=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_devhialm.pr") 
		if PriorityDevHI="LOW" or PriorityDevHI="HIGH" or PriorityDevHI="EMERGNCY" then
		    if PriorityDevHI="EMERGNCY" then PriorityDevHI="URGENT"
	    	sColor="AlarmStateIconFactory." & PriorityDevHI & "Color"
	    	Ind_DevHI.levelfillcolor=eval(sColor)
			Ind_DevHI.Style.visibility="inherit"
			TripDevHI=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_devhialm.tp")
		end if    
    	PriorityDevLO=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_devloalm.pr")
		if PriorityDevLO="LOW" or PriorityDevLO="HIGH" or PriorityDevLO="EMERGNCY" then
		    if PriorityDevLO="EMERGNCY" then PriorityDevLO="URGENT"
	    	sColor="AlarmStateIconFactory." & PriorityDevLO & "Color"
			Ind_DevLO.levelfillcolor=eval(sColor)
			Ind_DevLO.Style.visibility="inherit"
			TripDevLO=oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_devloalm.tp")
		end if
		Ind_DevHI.value=(cpRange-TripDevHI)*100/cpRange
    	Ind_DevLO.value=(cpRange-abs(TripDevLO))*100/cpRange
	end if
	if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_badctlfl")<>"" then
   		iBadCtlFl=oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_badctlfl")
   		if iBadCtlFl>0 then
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   		else
      		oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   		end if
    end if		
    if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HPM_DevBar_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

' Switches

sub HPM_Switch_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource) 
   dim iPV,oShape
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.value
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   if typename(oShape.objects("LinSw0")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("LinSw0").styleclass="LinSw0_" & index	   
   if typename(oShape.objects("LinSw1")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("LinSw1").styleclass="LinSw1_" & index	   
   if iPV=cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_BadPVState")) then
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   else
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " HPM_Switch_AlpPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description    
end sub

sub HPM_Switch3P_AlpPAR_OnUpdate(oSource) 
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	Set PolTL = oSource.ParentNode.ParentNode.Objects("PolTL")
	Set PolTR = oSource.ParentNode.ParentNode.Objects("PolTR")
	Set PolBL = oSource.ParentNode.ParentNode.Objects("PolBL")
	Set PolBR = oSource.ParentNode.ParentNode.Objects("PolBR")
	Set LinH = oSource.ParentNode.ParentNode.Objects("LinH")
	Set LinV = oSource.ParentNode.ParentNode.Objects("LinV")
	PolTL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_Off"
	PolTR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_Off"
	PolBL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_Off"
	PolBR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_Off"
	LinH.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_Off"
	LinV.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_Off"
	for i=1 to 3 
   		If ucase(oSource.value) = ucase(oShape.GetCustomProperty("Text", "cp_Value" & i)) or i=3  Then        	
			Select case UCase(oShape.GetCustomProperty("Text", "cp_Position" & i))
				Case "TL"
					PolTL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "TR"
					PolTR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "BL"
					PolBL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "BR"
					PolBR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "H"
					LinH.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "V"
					LinV.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "TLBR"
					PolTL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					PolBR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case "TRBL"
					PolTR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					PolBL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
				Case ""
				Case Else
					PolTL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					PolTR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					PolBL.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					PolBR.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					LinH.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"
					LinV.styleClass = "PolXX_Switch_ON"			
			end select
			exit for
		end if
	next		 
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " HPM_Switch3P_AlpPAR_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description    
end sub

'--------------------------------------
'Totalizers; 
'--------------------------------------
sub HPM_Totalizer_TVDEV_OnUpdate(oSource)
   dim oShape
   'on error resume next
   iTV=oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_acctv")
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   if oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_acctvfl")=0 then
   		for i = 1 to 2
   			if oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_accdevfl" & i)=1 and oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_acctp" & i)< iTV then
				iTV = oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_acctp" & i)
				iInd=i
			end if
   		next
		oShape.objects("TxtAccTvDevFl").innertext = oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_AccDev" & iInd &"Text")
	else
		oShape.objects("TxtAccTvDevFl").innertext = oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_AccTvText")
	end if
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Text","cp_TagnameEU")="" then oShape.objects("TxtEUDesc").innertext = oSource.DataValue("tagname.cp_eudesc")
   if err.number <>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " CDA_Totalizer_TVDEV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

sub HPM_Procop_Alp_OnUpdate(oSource)
	on error resume next
	set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
	state=oSource.value
	prcmod = oSource.datavalue("tagname.cp_procmod")
	
	set oSeqState=oSHape.objects("seqState")
	set oSeqSts=oSHape.objects("seqExecSts")
	set oFrame=oSHape.objects("PlgFrame")
	oSeqSts.value =1
	oSeqState.value =1
	if (oSource.quality and iQualityGood)<>iQualityGood then 
		oSeqState.value =2
	else
		oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame"
		select case state
			case 0,1,2 oSeqState.value=1
			case 3 oSeqState.value=12
            case 4 oSeqState.value=10:oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame_Running"
			case 5 oSeqSts.value =6:oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame_Running"
			case 6 oSeqSts.value =5:oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame_Running"
			case 7 oSeqState.value=3:oFrame.styleclass="ProcFrame_Running"
	    end select 
	end if
	if prcmod=4 then 
		oSeqState.value=9
	elseif prcmod=5 or prcmod=6 then 
		oSeqState.value=11
	end if

	if oShape.style.pixelwidth <>196 then
		iLeft = oShape.objects("ScTxtSelectBox").style.pixelleft
		iWidth = oShape.objects("ScTxtSelectBox").style.pixelwidth
		oShape.objects("RecFrame").style.pixelleft = iLeft+2
		oShape.objects("RecFrame").style.pixelwidth = iWidth-4
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").style.pixelleft = iLeft+6
		iY1 = oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(1).y
		iY2 = oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(2).y
		iY3 = oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(3).y
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(3).setnodepoint iWidth-18,iY3
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(2).setnodepoint iWidth-6,iY2
		oShape.objects("PlgFrame").nodes(1).setnodepoint iWidth-18,iY1
		oShape.objects("seqState").style.pixelleft = iLeft+7
		oShape.objects("seqState").style.pixelwidth = 16
		oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").style.pixelleft = iLeft+iWidth-33
		oShape.objects("ConAlarmState").style.pixelwidth = 22
		oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelleft = iLeft+iWidth-56
		oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelwidth = 16
		oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelleft = iLeft+24
		oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelwidth = oShape.objects("seqExecSts").style.pixelleft-oShape.objects("TxtNameSeq").style.pixelleft
	end if
	if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage "HPM_Procop_Alp_OnUpdate->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

'Louvers

sub HPM_Louvers_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource) 
   dim iPV,oShape,iOP,index,indexOP
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.value
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   if typename(oShape.objects("LnClose")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("LnClose").styleclass="LnCLose_" & index	   
   if typename(oShape.objects("LnOpen")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("LnOpen").styleclass="LnOpen_" & index
   if typename(oShape.objects("LvrInbet")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("LvrInbet").styleclass="LvrInbet_" & index
   if typename(oShape.objects("LvrBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("LvrBody").styleclass="LvrBody_" & index 	
   if iPV=cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_BadPVState")) then
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   else
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   end if
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_op")<>"" then
   		iOP = oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_op")
		indexOP = oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iOP)
		if typename(oShape.objects("LvrMFold")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("LvrMfold").styleclass = "LvrMfold_" & indexOP
   end if
   if err.number<>0 and window.external.environment = 0 then window.external.LogMessage " HPM_Louvers_AlpGPV_OnUpdate ->" & oSource.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'-------------------------------------------------------
'MO Fans
'-------------------------------------------------------
sub HPM_MOFan_AlpPV_OnUpdate(oSource) 
   dim iPV,oShape,iOP,index,indexOP
   on error resume next
   set oShape=oSource.parentnode.parentnode
   iPV=oSource.value
   index=oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iPV)
   if typename(oShape.objects("OvlBody")) <> "Nothing" then oShape.objects("OvlBody").styleclass="OvlBody_" & index	   
   if iPV=cint(oShape.getcustomproperty("Num","cp_BadPVState")) then
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_Bad"
   else
      oShape.objects("TxtBad").styleclass="TxtBad_NotBad"
   end if
   if oShape.getcustomproperty("Parameter","cp_op")<>"" then
   		iOP = oSource.datavalue("Tagname.cp_op")
		indexOP = oShape.getCustomProperty("Style","cp_styleForPV" & iOP)

   end if

==================================================
FILE: Trend_Sup_GSH.sha
==================================================

<?xml version="1.0"?><shapefile version="1" type="dynamic" title="" description="" useFirstShapeForBadValue="0" width="106px" height="670px"><versionheaders><versionheader name="PS" value="6.51"/></versionheaders><scripts><script event="onshapeload" language="VBScript" id=""><![CDATA[on error resume next:Page.SetAttribute "Trend_Sup_GSH",me.id]]></script><script event="" language="VBScript" id="97178B2E-E690-4E91-A997-EABDF1C194A6"><![CDATA['COPYRIGHT (c) 2004-2010
'HONEYWELL INTL.,
'ALL RIGHTS RESERVED

'This shape provides the ability to view, edit and invoke trend groups.
dim tid				'Timer ID
dim bGroupSelected	'Used if All_TrdSel_lib_h_01_R300.sha is missing to ensure the operator responce
dim sParameter		'Used to store the parameter to be added in the selected trend group
dim bLoaded			'Used to to ensure once off update of the chart shape if present 
dim oWindow_Tnd			'Used to reload trend in SafeView Environment when trace added
dim selrecoffset	'Used to reload trend in SafeView Environment when trace added
dim bTrendSet		'Used to set the trend number to the last selected trend

'Check the number of empty pens left in the currently selected trend group. Called within AlpTrendGrp.onupdate
sub is_empty
	dim tagn, e_pl, pointpar, oShape, oShape_sel
	on error resume next
	if isnull(page.getattribute("TrdSel")) then exit sub
	set oshape=document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH"))
	set oshape_sel=document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("trdsel"))
	if oShape.objects("alpisprotected").value = 1 and window.external.securitylevel<4 then 
   		oShape_sel.title = "Protected"
		if typename(oShape_sel.objects("TxtFreePens"))<>"Nothing" then oShape_sel.objects("TxtFreePens").value="Protected"
		if oshape.objects("TrdTbl").style.visibility = "visible" then oshape.objects("RecProtected").style.pixelwidth=400
		exit sub		 
	else
		oshape.objects("RecProtected").style.pixelwidth=0
	end if	
	e_pl =0
	for i= 0 to 31   
		pointpar = oShape.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Point.value").getvalue(i)
		if pointpar=" " then e_pl=e_pl+1
	next
	oShape_sel.title = "Free Pens " & e_pl
	if typename(oShape_sel.objects("TxtFreePens"))<>"Nothing" then oShape_sel.objects("TxtFreePens").value="Free Pens " & e_pl
	if err.number <> 0 then window.external.LogMessage "is_empty ->" & oShape.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

'Update the contents of the chart shape object with the pens in the currently selected trend group. Called within AlpTrendGrp.onupdate
sub upd_chart
	dim oShape
	on error resume next
	if isnull(page.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH")) then exit sub
	if isnull(page.getattribute("TrdSel")) then exit sub
	set oshape=document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH"))
	for each oObj in page.all.tags("DIV")
  		if oObj.classname="hsc.shape.1" then
     		if not (oObj.objects("Custtrd") is nothing) then
				for i=0 to 31
	      			point = oshape.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Point.value").getvalue(i)
		  			par = oshape.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Parameter.value").getvalue(i)		  							
					if oObj.objects("CustTrd").legend.PointID(i+1)<>"" then oObj.objects("CustTrd").legend.PointID(i+1)=" "					
					oObj.objects("CustTrd").legend.parameter(i+1)=""
					if asc(left(point,1))<>32 then
		     			oObj.objects("CustTrd").legend.PointID(i+1)=point
		     			oObj.objects("CustTrd").legend.parameter(i+1)=par
						oObj.objects("CustTrd").legend.PlotVisible(i+1)=true
		  			end if
				next    
     		end if
  		end if 
	next
	if err.number <> 0 then window.external.LogMessage "upd_chart ->" & oShape.id & " " & err.description 
end sub

'Prompt to select a trend group in case All_TrdSel.sha is not present in the display
sub trdresp
	on error resume next
	if bCancel=true then 
    	window.clearinterval(tid)
		bCancel=false
		bTrdResp=false
		bGroupSelected = false
    	exit sub
	end if
	istatus=window.external.getresponse3 ( "No trend group selected! Please select one. ", 0, resp)
	if istatus=1 and resp<>"" then 
		trdg = csng(resp)
		if err.number = 0 and trdg > 0 then 	
			set oshape_TrendSup=document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH"))
			oshape_TrendSup.objects("AlpTrendGrp").value = trdg
		else
    		window.external.messagezonetext "this is not a valid trend number!"
			istatus=0
		end if
		bTrdResp=false
		bGroupSelected = true
    	window.clearinterval (tid)
		call add2trend(sParameter)
	end if
	if istatus=0 then window.clearinterval (tid)
	if err.number <> 0 then window.external.LogMessage "trdresp ->" & oShape.id & " " & err.description
end sub

'Add the parameter stored in sParameter to the selected trend group
sub add2trend(sparam)
	dim oshape, oshape_c, oshape_sel,cm_point, cm_param, trd_num, empty_place, pointpar, tagn
	on error resume next
	set oshape_sel=document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("trdsel"))
	selrecoffset=oshape_sel.objects("AlpTrendGrp").value
	set oshape = document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("lastselection")).parentnode.parentnode
	set oshape_c=document.getelementbyid(page.getattribute("Trend_Sup_GSH"))
	Trd_Par= oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter",sParam)
	DotLoc= Instr(1, Trd_Par,".")
	sPoint_CP= Mid(Trd_Par,1, DotLoc-1)
	sParameter_CP = Mid(Trd_Par, DotLoc+1, 24)
	cm_point = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Point",sPoint_CP)
	if cm_point = "" then cm_point = sPoint_cp
	cm_param = oShape.GetCustomProperty("Parameter",sParameter_CP)
	if cm_param = "" then cm_param = sParameter_CP
	trd_num = oShape_c.objects("AlpTrendGrp").value
	if isnull(page.getattribute("TrdSel")) and bGroupSelected = false then 
		sParameter = sparam 
		page.focus
		bTrdResp=true
		tid = window.setinterval ("trdresp",500)
    	exit sub
	end if
	if oShape_c.objects("alpisprotected").value = 1 and window.external.securitylevel<4 then 
		window.external.messagezonetext "The trend group no " & trd_num & " is protected!"
		exit sub
	end if	 
	exist = 0
	empty_place = -1
	for i=0 to 31
		pointpar = oShape_c.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Point.value").getvalue(i)
		if ucase(pointpar) = ucase (cm_point) and ucase(oShape_c.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Parameter.value").getvalue(i)) = ucase (cm_param) then exist = 1	
		if pointpar=" " and empty_place = -1 then empty_place = i 
	next
	if exist = 0 then
		if empty_place >= 0 then
			oShape_c.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Point.value").setvalue empty_place, cm_point
			oShape_c.objects("TrdTbl").fields.item("Parameter.value").setvalue empty_place, cm_param
			free = right(oShape_sel.title,2)
			free=free-1
        	oShape_sel.title="Free Pens " & free
			if typename(oShape_sel.objects("TxtFreePens"))<>"Nothing" then oShape_sel.objects("TxtFreePens").value="Free Pens " & free
			window.external.messagezonetext ucase(cm_point) & "." & ucase(cm_param) & " Has been added to trend group no " & trd_num
			set oSview=getobject("","Honeywell.Workspace.Client")
			If oSview.IsManagerActive = true then
				reload_trend=false
				reload_popup=false
				for each oWnd in window.external.parent.StationWindows
					recoffset=oWnd.currentrecordoffset
					currpage=oWnd.currentpage 
					if lcase(currpage)="systnd03.htm" and cint(recoffset)=cint(selrecoffset) then                      
					   reload_trend=true
					   set oWindow_Tnd=oWnd										   
					elseif lcase(currpage)="chart_popup_cp.htm" then
						if cint(selrecoffset)=cint(mid(oWnd.document.title,14)) then 
					   		reload_popup=true
					   		set oWindow_Pop=oWnd					
						end if
					end if
				next
				if reload_trend=true then 
					oSview.setoutputfocuswnd(oWindow_Tnd.AppWindow.HWND)
					window.setTimeout "reloadTrend", 1000 
				end if	
				if reload_popup=true then 
					oSview.setoutputfocuswnd(oWindow_Pop.AppWindow.HWND)
					window.setTimeout "reloadPopup", 1000 
				end if
			else
				set chrt_pop=hdxpopupbehavior.findpopup("dummy" & selrecoffset)
				if typename(chrt_pop)<>"Nothing" then
					chrt_pop.close
					window.setTimeout "reloadPopup", 1000
				end if
			end if	
		else 
			window.external.messagezonetext "Trend group no " & trd_num & " is full!"
		end if
	else
		window.external.messagezonetext ucase(cm_point) & "." & ucase(cm_param) & " Has been already configured in trend group no " & trd_num 
	end if
	window.settimeout "upd_chart", 1000
	bGroupSelected = false
	if err.number <> 0 then window.external.LogMessage "add2trend ->" & oShape.id & " " & sparam & " " & err.description
end sub

sub reloadTrend()
	oWindow_Tnd.requestserverlrn 21, 8, selrecoffset
end sub

sub reloadPopup()
	call trd_popup
end sub]]></script></scripts><parameters><parameter name="Revision" type="Num" description="" defaultvalue="R400 Rev.0"/></parameters><shapes><shape><group><element><html><content><![CDATA[
